UI-PARITY-002lowparityfixedA dragged marker, tempo point or time signature flag jumps back to its old place for a moment after release

docs/bugs/reports/UI-PARITY-002-a-dragged-marker-tempo-point-or-time-signature-f.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/timeline/MarkerRow.svelte:73-79 (up: drag = null before moveMarker is answered)
ui/src/components/timeline/TempoBand.svelte:116-121 (laneUp: pointDrag = null, then moveTempoPoint)
ui/src/components/timeline/TempoBand.svelte:183-188 (rowUp: flagDrag = null, then moveMeterChange)
Siblings that keep the dragged look until the answer: ui/src/components/automation/AutoLane.svelte:68-79, 153-164 (pending until current.rev >= rev), ui/src/components/Arrange.svelte:1436-1439 (clipPending), ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:375-386 and CcLanes.svelte:160-172 (pending), ui/src/components/comp/CompBand.svelte:46-54, 116-118, ui/src/components/arrange/AudioClipHandles.svelte:50-61, 243.

What happens

On release of a marker, a tempo point or a time signature flag, the drag state is cleared at once and the intent is sent. Until core's answer (the new timeline with its rev) comes back, the part is drawn from the old timeline, so the marker / point / flag is drawn at its original place for that time and then jumps to the new place. Every other drag part keeps the dragged look until the answer arrives.

What should happen

The sibling parts do this on purpose; the comment at AutoLane.svelte:68 says: "The look kept after release and sending the intent, until the answer arrives (so the point does not jump back to the original place for a moment)". The tempo / marker parts are the same kind of drag (CLAUDE.md: only the screen moves first, one intent on release) and should not flash back either.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run. Drag a marker two beats to the right and release. Between the release and the next state from core (one or more frames, longer when core is busy, for example while a song with plugins plays), the tag is drawn at the old tick. The same for a tempo point and a time signature flag.

Cause

up, laneUp and rowUp set the drag to null before sending and keep nothing; the shown positions (tags in MarkerRow, T.draggedPoints(points, pointDrag) and flagsShown in TempoBand) then fall back to timeline as it was before the move.

Suggested fix

Keep the released drag as pending and use it for the shown positions until current.rev reaches the rev that send(...) resolves with (the same pattern as AutoLane.svelte:153-164 and its $effect at 74-77); clear it on a new press and when the marker / point / flag is gone. No protocol or look change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run; the length of the jump was not measured). MarkerRow.svelte:73-79 up, TempoBand.svelte:116-121 laneUp and 183-188 rowUp set the drag to null and then send; the shown places come from timeline as it was until core's answer arrives, so the thing is drawn at its old place for those frames. The other drag parts keep the look until the answer (AutoLane pending, Arrange clipPending, Roll / CcLanes pending, CompBand).

Fix plan: the same kept look in ui/src/components/timeline/MarkerRow.svelte and TempoBand.svelte: keep the released drag until current.rev reaches the rev send() resolves with; drop it on a new press, on Esc, and when the marker / point / flag it names is gone. Write it so it cannot fail the way UI-ARRANGE-001 does: the kept look must be tied to the thing by its name (marker id; tempo point tick; flag bar), and used only while that thing is still in timeline, never by an index. Note Rust snaps the sent tick (architecture 21-15) and reselects the moved point; with UI-ARRANGE-006 fixed the kept place equals the answered one.

Fix

Not committed yet (the lead commits).

  • ui/src/components/timeline/MarkerRow.svelte, ui/src/components/timeline/TempoBand.svelte: the released drag is kept (pending, pointPending, flagPending) until current.rev reaches the rev send() resolves with, the same pattern as AutoLane / CompBand. It is tied to the thing by its name and used only while that thing is still as it was in timeline: the marker id at its old tick, the tempo point's tick with its old tempo, the flag's bar. A new press drops it; Esc does not (as in the siblings: the intent is already sent).

Tests: ui/tests/round6.timeline.test.ts — "after release a marker keeps its moved place until core's answer; the answer or a rev with no change ends it", "after release a tempo point and a time signature flag keep their moved place until core's answer".

UI-PARITY-007lowbugfixedAfter an unreadable Enter, the mixer, metronome and percent typing boxes no longer close when focus leaves

docs/bugs/reports/UI-PARITY-007-after-an-unreadable-enter-the-mixer-metronome-an.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/params/ValueField.svelte:27-31 (done = true is set before onsubmit, whatever onsubmit does)
ui/src/components/params/ValueField.svelte:42-44 (onblur cancels only when !done)
ui/src/components/mixer/control.svelte.ts:121-124 (submit returns false and keeps typing on for text that cannot be read; fader, pan and both sends)
ui/src/components/TrackHeads.svelte:48-50 (track head dB: returns early, box stays)
ui/src/components/MetronomeWindow.svelte:153-156 (metronome volume: "Text that cannot be read keeps the box open")
ui/src/components/pianoroll/PercentSlider.svelte:83-85 (quantize strength and swing: returns early, box stays)

What happens

In the value box of a fader, pan, send, the track head dB, the metronome volume or the quantize strength / swing, type text that cannot be read (for example 「あ」) and press Enter: the box stays open, as meant (7-readings A-18 "Text that cannot be read keeps the box open"). From then on the box never closes by leaving it: pressing anywhere else moves the focus away, but the box stays on the screen, with the typed text, and the number under it is hidden. The only ways out are to press the box again and use Esc or a readable Enter. The same box closes on a press outside when Enter was never pressed.

What should happen

ValueField's own header says "Leaving the box by pressing elsewhere also stops (only Enter decides)". The sibling boxes close on blur in every case: NumberField.svelte:85 (onblur={() => close(false)}), timeline/ValueInput.svelte:66 (onblur={close}), pianoroll/NoteFieldInput.svelte:65 (onblur={oncancel}), even after an unusable Enter (they never set a "done" flag).

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run.

  1. Open the mixer, double-click a fader's dB text (or Tab to the fader and press Enter).
  2. Type 「あ」 and press Enter: ValueField.onkeydown sets done = true, calls onsubmit; parseDb("あ") is undefined, submit returns false, typing stays true.
  3. Press somewhere else in the mixer: onblur runs, done is true, so oncancel is not called. The box stays.

The same with the pan text, a send text, the dB in a track head, the metronome volume number and the quantize strength / swing value.

Cause

done is meant to stop the blur that follows a decided Enter from also cancelling. It is set before the parent says whether the text was taken, and it is never set back when the parent keeps the box open. Parents that always close on submit (ParamRow, ClipPanel, ClipsPanel, SettingsDialog latency) do not meet this; the five parents above keep it open for unreadable text.

Suggested fix

Let onsubmit return whether it closed the box (the mixer's submit already returns a boolean; make the others return one too) and set done only when it returns true; or set done = false again after an onsubmit that leaves the box mounted. No protocol, wording or look change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). ValueField.svelte:27-31 sets done = true before calling onsubmit, and onblur (42-44) cancels only when !done; the parents that keep the box open for unreadable text (mixControl.submit returns false and leaves typing on, control.svelte.ts:118-125; TrackHeads.svelte:48-50; MetronomeWindow.svelte:153-156; PercentSlider.svelte:83-85; 7-readings A-18: "Text that cannot be read keeps the box open") leave the same ValueField mounted with done stuck at true, so a later blur closes nothing. The header of ValueField.svelte says leaving the box stops.

Severity lowered to low: it needs an unreadable Enter first, and Esc or a readable Enter still closes the box; nothing in the song is touched.

Fix plan: in ui/src/components/params/ValueField.svelte set done back to false after onsubmit returns when the field is still mounted (the smallest change, no parent changes), or let onsubmit return whether it closed. Test: unreadable Enter, then blur → oncancel is called.

Fix

ui/src/components/params/ValueField.svelte: after Enter, when the box is still there after the next tick (text that could not be read), done goes back to false, so leaving the box stops it. No parent changed. Test: ui/tests/fix-ui-values.test.ts "UI-PARITY-007: after an Enter that could not be read, leaving the box closes it".

UI-PARITY-008lowparityfixedPressing outside a note list, CC list or selected-notes field box pulls the focus back to the row, unlike the number field

docs/bugs/reports/UI-PARITY-008-pressing-outside-a-note-list-cc-list-or-selected.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteFieldInput.svelte:65 (onblur={oncancel}: the blur and Esc call the same handler)
ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteListPanel.svelte:173-175 (cancel → close(true)), :134-140 (close(true) focuses the row [data-at] after tick())
ui/src/components/pianoroll/CcListPanel.svelte:157-159, :125-131 (the same)
ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteInspector.svelte:347 (oncancel={() => ... closeField(true)}), :101-105 (queueMicrotask(() => fieldButtons[name]?.focus()))
The sibling that does it right: ui/src/components/NumberField.svelte:85 (onblur={() => close(false)}; only Enter and Esc call close(true))

What happens

While a field of the note list, the CC list or 「選んだノート」 is being typed in, pressing another text box (the shelf search, the params panel search, a box in the export dialog) does not leave the focus there. The blur of the note field closes it and, a microtask later, focuses the list row (or the field button in 「選んだノート」) again. That happens inside the focus change, so the pressed box does not get the focus (or loses it at once) and the person has to press it a second time. Keys typed meanwhile go to the list row and the document's key handler (one-letter keys act as shortcuts).

What should happen

Esc puts the focus back on the row (spec 3 "Note list": Enter decides, Esc stops; the focus stays in the list for the next Enter), but a press outside should only close the box, as the transport's number field does: NumberField.svelte closes with close(false) on blur and close(true) only on Enter / Esc. The arrange and tempo boxes (timeline/ValueInput.svelte:66) and ValueField (params/ValueField.svelte:42-44) also do not take the focus back on blur.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run.

  1. Open the piano roll with the note list, double-click a note's 「Vel」 field.
  2. With the box open, press the search box of the params panel (or of the shelf).
  3. The input's blur runs oncancel → cancel(token) → close(true); after tick() the row button [data-at] gets focus(). In WebKit the microtasks run when the blur listener returns, so the focus move to the search box is blocked (or the row takes the focus right after). The search box does not have the caret.

The same in the CC list and in the four fields at the top of 「選んだノート」.

Cause

NoteFieldInput has one oncancel for both Esc and blur, and all three parents answer it with "close and focus the row / field again". NumberField tells the two apart (close(true) for keys, close(false) for blur).

Suggested fix

Give NoteFieldInput a separate blur path (for example onblur={() => oncancel(false)} with a refocus argument, Esc passing true), and in the three parents call close(false) / closeField(false) for the blur. No protocol, wording or look change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run; the browser's order of events was not tried in WebKitGTK). NoteFieldInput.svelte:65 uses oncancel for blur as well as Esc; NoteListPanel.svelte:173-175 answers with close(true), which focuses the row after tick(); CcListPanel.svelte and NoteInspector.svelte (queueMicrotask(() => fieldButtons[name]?.focus())) do the same. Whatever the order of the browser's focus steps, the row's focus() runs after the blur, so the box the person pressed does not keep the focus (either the move is stopped, or the row takes it right after). NumberField.svelte tells the two apart (close(false) on blur, close(true) on Enter / Esc).

Fix plan (as suggested): give the blur its own path: in ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteFieldInput.svelte call oncancel(false) on blur and oncancel(true) on Esc (or a second prop), and in NoteListPanel.svelte, CcListPanel.svelte, NoteInspector.svelte pass that on to close(refocus) / closeField(refocus). Tests: blur → the box closes and document.activeElement is not the row.

Fix

No protocol, wording or look change.

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteFieldInput.svelte: oncancel(refocus): Esc passes true, the blur false.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteListPanel.svelte, ui/src/components/pianoroll/CcListPanel.svelte, ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteInspector.svelte: pass it on to close(refocus) / closeField(refocus), so a press outside only closes the box.
  • Tests: ui/tests/pianoroll.panels.test.ts "pressing outside a field box leaves the focus there (UI-PARITY-008)" (note list, selected notes); ui/tests/pianoroll.r5-notes.test.ts "pressing another text box closes the box and leaves the focus there (UI-PARITY-008)".
UI-PARITY-009lowparityfixedTyped dB, pan, metronome volume and sound knob values do not read full-width digits, unlike the other number boxes

docs/bugs/reports/UI-PARITY-009-typed-db-pan-metronome-volume-and-sound-knob-val.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/mixer/scale.ts:63-68 (parseDb: only − and ー are mapped; \d is ASCII only)
ui/src/components/mixer/scale.ts:71-78 (parsePan)
ui/src/components/metronome.ts:59-63 (parseClickDb)
ui/src/components/params/knobRows.ts:41-45 (typedKnob: Number("３５") is NaN)
Users of these: mixer/control.svelte.ts:172 (fader), mixer/SendBar.svelte:22, mixer/PanKnob.svelte:22, TrackHeads.svelte:49 (track head dB), params/ClipPanel.svelte:124 and params/ClipsPanel.svelte:45 (clip gain), MetronomeWindow.svelte:155, params/ParamRow.svelte:177 (the four sound knobs).

What happens

With the Japanese input on, digits come in full width. 「－３」 typed into a fader, a send or the track head dB, 「Ｌ２０」 or 「２０」 into the pan, 「－１２」 into the metronome volume, 「－２」 into a clip's 「ゲイン」, and 「３５」 into a sound knob (「明るさ」 …) cannot be read: the mixer and metronome boxes stay open, the clip gain and the knob send nothing and say nothing. The same digits typed in the transport's tempo, the position, the note and CC lists, the clip's position, length, fade, pitch and 「音程を寄せる」, the latency box and a built-in effect's value are read.

What should happen

The other number boxes turn the text to ASCII first: valueClamp.ts:23 and time.ts:64 (normalize("NFKC")), pianoroll/noteFields.ts:27, params/clipFields.ts:23 (the clip panel's own other fields), SettingsDialog.svelte:79, clipEdits.ts:42 (the repeat count), and Rust's ParamSpec::read for built-in effects (architecture 20-13: "full-width digits and 「−」 too"). 7-readings writes the same rule for the position box ("full-width characters are fine"), the repeat count ("full-width digits read") and track numbers ("Full-width digits are read as digits ... people type with the Japanese input on"). In the clip panel the gain is the only field of its group that does not read them.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run.

  1. Turn the Japanese input on (full-width digits).
  2. Double-click a fader's dB text in the mixer, type -3: the box holds 「－３」.
  3. Enter: parseDb keeps 「－３」 (U+FF0D and U+FF13 are not mapped), the pattern ^[+-]?(\d+...)$ fails, undefined; the box stays open.
  4. In the params panel of a built-in sound, double-click 「明るさ」's value, type 35 (「３５」), Enter: typedKnob gives null, nothing is sent and nothing is said; the same text in a reverb's 「ミックス」 row is read by Rust.

Cause

These four parsers were written with their own small character maps and never call normalize("NFKC"), unlike the shared tidy helpers of the other fields.

Suggested fix

Start each of parseDb, parsePan, parseClickDb and typedKnob with text.normalize("NFKC") (as clipFields.ts tidy does; keep the − mapping after it, since NFKC leaves U+2212 as it is). No protocol, wording or look change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). parseDb and parsePan (mixer/scale.ts:63-78), parseClickDb (metronome.ts:59-63) and typedKnob (params/knobRows.ts:41-45) map only 「−」 (and 「ー」) and then use \d / Number, which do not read full-width digits; the other boxes call normalize("NFKC") first (valueClamp.ts:23, time.ts, noteFields.ts, clipFields.ts). 7-readings says full-width is read for the position box, the repeat count and track numbers ("people type with the Japanese input on").

Fix plan (as suggested): start each of the four parsers with text.normalize("NFKC") (keep the 「−」 → "-" mapping after it: NFKC leaves U+2212; it does turn U+FF0D 「－」 into "-"). Tests beside the existing parser tests: 「－３」, 「Ｌ２０」, 「－１２」, 「３５」. Touches the same submit path as UI-MIX-005; do them in one pass.

Fix

parseDb, parsePan (ui/src/components/mixer/scale.ts), parseClickDb (ui/src/components/metronome.ts) and typedKnob (ui/src/components/params/knobRows.ts) start with normalize("NFKC"). Test: ui/tests/fix-ui-values.test.ts "UI-PARITY-009: full-width digits".

UI-PARITY-010lowparityfixedThe transport's tempo and time signature boxes open and take Enter while counting in or recording, while the tempo band's boxes do not

docs/bugs/reports/UI-PARITY-010-the-transport-s-tempo-and-time-signature-boxes-o.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/Transport.svelte:446-470 (the tempo and time signature NumberFields get no busy check)
ui/src/components/Transport.svelte:294-297 (commitClamped sends ClampValue after the set command, whatever Rust does with it)
ui/src/components/NumberField.svelte:89-98 (the reading is always m-typable and always opens)
The sibling that does it right: ui/src/components/timeline/TempoBand.svelte:281-287 and :333-340 (class:m-typable={!busy}, if (!busy) editing = ...; its own comment at :29 "While recording or during count-in: only chooses (the core accepts only that)")
Rust side: crates/meloet-core/src/play/timeline.rs:192-195 and :222-224 (set_tempo / set_meter return at once when busy())

What happens

While counting in or recording, the tempo and time signature numbers in the transport still light up on hover as a place to type (m-typable), open the input box when pressed, and take Enter. Rust drops the SetTempo / SetMeter, so nothing changes. If the typed value was out of range (「400」), the screen also sends ClampValue and the status line says the tempo was set to 300.00, though nothing was set. The same values in the tempo band (a tempo point's number, a time signature flag) do not look typable and do not open at that time.

What should happen

The same value should behave the same in both places: not typable while counting in or recording, as TempoBand.svelte does and as Rust enforces. 7-readings line 77: the typable look is "Not while the number cannot be typed".

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run.

  1. Press ● to record (or be in the count-in).
  2. Press the tempo number in the transport: the box opens.
  3. Type 400 and Enter: readTempo clamps to 300 and commitClamped sends setTempo (dropped by set_tempo because busy()) and clampValue (Rust shows ValueClamped, the "set to the nearest end" text of spec 6).
  4. Compare: double-click a tempo point's number in the tempo band during the recording: nothing opens.

Cause

Transport passes no "busy" state to its NumberFields; NumberField has no way to be shown as not typable.

Suggested fix

Give NumberField a disabled prop (no m-typable, open does nothing; the reading looks as now) and pass recordingNow for the tempo and the time signature in Transport.svelte, as TempoBand does with busy. No protocol or wording change; the reading's look while busy is that of a plain readout (what TempoBand shows).

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). Transport.svelte:446-470: the tempo and time signature NumberFields get no busy state, and NumberField.svelte:90 always has m-typable and opens; set_tempo / set_meter return None at once while busy() (crates/meloet-core/src/play/timeline.rs:192-195, 222-224), and commitClamped (Transport.svelte:294-297) still sends clampValue, so for an out-of-range text the status line says the value was set though nothing was. The tempo band's boxes are not typable while busy. 7-readings line 77 (hover of typable numbers): not while the number cannot be typed.

One root with UI-ROLL-008 (which now holds UI-PARITY-005), UI-ARRANGE-009 and UI-ARRANGE-013 (parts that look usable while recording or counting in though Rust refuses).

Fix plan (as suggested): a disabled prop on ui/src/components/NumberField.svelte (no m-typable, open does nothing, the reading looks as now), passed as the transport's recording / count-in state for the tempo and the time signature in ui/src/components/Transport.svelte. Check the position box (locate is refused while busy too, play.rs:928-931) and give it the same prop if it opens today. No protocol or wording change.

Fix

Not committed yet (the lead commits).

  • ui/src/components/NumberField.svelte (another fixer's file, kept small): a disabled prop — pressing does not open the box, no m-typable, no hover border, cursor: default, and aria-disabled="true" (the transport's way of marking parts that cannot be used now). Without it the markup is as before.
  • ui/src/components/Transport.svelte: disabled={recordingNow} for the tempo and the time signature, and for the position too (locate is refused while busy as well, as the verification asked).

Test: ui/tests/transport.test.ts — "while recording or counting in, tempo, time signature and position do not open and do not look typable (core takes none of them, as in the tempo band)".

UI-PARITY-012lowparityfixedUp and down in a built-in effect's parameter list can choose a row out of view; the plugin list scrolls to it

docs/bugs/reports/UI-PARITY-012-up-and-down-in-a-built-in-effect-s-parameter-lis.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/params/BuiltinList.svelte:109-123 (onkeydown: ↑ ↓ call pick(next) only)
ui/src/components/params/BuiltinList.svelte:75-78 (pick focuses the list with preventScroll: true and sends selectParam; nothing scrolls)
ui/src/components/params/BuiltinList.svelte:129 (the rows are in a ScrollArea with no bound viewport)
The sibling that does it right: ui/src/components/params/PluginList.svelte:105-113 (reveal) and :167-171 (pick(next); reveal(next);)

What happens

In the params panel of a built-in effect with more rows than fit (the 4-band EQ has 10 rows plus group headings; with the preset list open or a low window, fewer fit), ↑ ↓ move the chosen row (gray.4, accent line) past the bottom or the top of the visible part. The list does not scroll, so the chosen row is out of view and the person does not see which one Enter or ← → will act on. A plugin's parameter list scrolls so the chosen row stays visible. The note list, the CC list and the history band also keep the row in view (NoteListPanel.svelte:126, CcListPanel.svelte:108, history/HistoryList.svelte:92).

What should happen

The same as PluginList: after ↑ ↓ the chosen row is scrolled into view. Spec 2 "Scroll bar" names parameters among the lists that scroll this way, and spec 4 gives the same ↑ ↓ for "In the parameters" whatever the effect is.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run.

  1. Make the window low (1024 × 640) and open a lower band so the right rail is short, or open 「プリセット ▾」 above the rows.
  2. Put a 4-band EQ on a track and choose it; click the first row.
  3. Press ↓ several times: sel.row moves to rows below the visible part; the ScrollArea's scrollTop never changes.

Cause

BuiltinList was not given the reveal step PluginList has (PluginList binds the ScrollArea's viewport and sets scrollTop; BuiltinList does not bind it).

Suggested fix

Bind the ScrollArea's viewport in BuiltinList and, after pick(next) from a key, scroll the row [data-param="…"] into view (for example el.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" }), as EndpointRow.svelte:32 does, or a reveal like PluginList's). No protocol, wording or look change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). BuiltinList.svelte onkeydown ends with pick(next); pick focuses the list with preventScroll: true and sends selectParam; the file has no reveal (grep), while PluginList.svelte:106 has reveal and calls it after pick(next) (170). So a row chosen with ↑ ↓ can lie outside the visible part of the ScrollArea.

Fix plan (as suggested): in ui/src/components/params/BuiltinList.svelte bind the ScrollArea's viewport and, after pick(next) from a key, bring the row into view with the same reveal as PluginList (share one small function rather than a second copy). Check SoundKnobList.svelte needs nothing (four rows).

Fix

ui/src/components/params/BuiltinList.svelte: after ↑ ↓ choose a row, the row [data-param] is brought into view with scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" }) (as EndpointRow.svelte does; PluginList's reveal counts virtual rows, so it is not shared). SoundKnobList.svelte has four rows and needs nothing. Test: ui/tests/params.test.ts "UI-PARITY-012: ↑ ↓ bring the chosen row into view".

UI-PARITY-013lowparityfixedThe quantize strength and swing bars show no gray.7 start mark while dragged, unlike the parameter and metronome bars

docs/bugs/reports/UI-PARITY-013-the-quantize-strength-and-swing-bars-show-no-gra.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/pianoroll/PercentSlider.svelte:47-53 (ondown does not keep the start value) and :122-124 (the bar draws only the track, the fill and the knob)
Siblings that have it: ui/src/components/params/ParamRow.svelte:62, :107, :228 (from, and {#if dragging}<div class="knob ghost" …> with a gray.7 border, :359-363) and ui/src/components/MetronomeWindow.svelte:44, :85, :267 (the same, :401-404)

What happens

While the 「強さ」 or 「スウィング」 bar of quantize in 「選んだノート」 is dragged, only the moving knob is drawn; where the value was before the drag is not shown. The same kind of bar in the params panel and in the metronome window shows a gray.7-framed knob at the start value for the whole drag.

What should happen

CLAUDE.md rules: "While dragging, only the screen moves first, and on release one intent is sent ... The original position is shown with a gray.7 border." PercentSlider's own header says it is the "smoothly moving value" of spec 2 "Parameter row", and 7-readings line 79 ("Hover and moving of the strength and swing bars") gives it the parameter row's moving look; ParamRow and MetronomeWindow, the other bars of that form, draw the start mark.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run.

  1. Open the piano roll and 「選んだノート」.
  2. Press the 「強さ」 bar of quantize and drag sideways: the knob follows; no gray.7 mark stays at the old value. Compare with dragging a parameter bar of a built-in effect.

Cause

PercentSlider keeps no from value and has no ghost knob element.

Suggested fix

Keep from = value in ondown and draw {#if dragging}<div class="knob ghost" style:left="{from}%"></div>{/if} with the same .knob.ghost style as ParamRow (border line.hair gray.7, no fill). Uses existing tokens only; no protocol or wording change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). PercentSlider.svelte keeps no start value in ondown (47-53) and draws only the track, the fill and the knob (no ghost, grep); ParamRow.svelte and MetronomeWindow.svelte draw a gray.7-framed knob at the start value while dragging. docs/bugs/reports/UI-PARITY-014-the-chosen-clip-panels-scroll-with-the-browser-s.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/params/ClipPanel.svelte:276 (<div class="list"> around the groups) and :466-469 (.list { min-height: 0; overflow-y: auto; }, no scroll bar hiding)
ui/src/components/params/ClipsPanel.svelte:117 and :186-189 (the same)

What happens

  3. The groups do not fit; .list gets the native vertical scroll bar.

Cause

Suggested fix

UI-PARITY-015lowparityfixedAfter Enter or Esc in a fader, pan, send or parameter value box the focus is lost, while the number field and note list give it back

docs/bugs/reports/UI-PARITY-015-after-enter-or-esc-in-a-fader-pan-send-or-parame.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/params/ValueField.svelte:25-41 (Enter and Esc call onsubmit / oncancel; the input is then removed and nothing gets the focus)
ui/src/components/mixer/control.svelte.ts:88-98 (Enter on a focused fader, pan or send opens the box), :118-125 and :139-141 (submit, stopTyping only turn typing off)
ui/src/components/params/BuiltinList.svelte:83-88, SoundKnobList.svelte:51-54, PluginList.svelte:129-131 (Enter on the chosen row opens the box); params/paramUi.svelte.ts:15-19 (stopTyping only clears the row)
Siblings that do it right: ui/src/components/NumberField.svelte:41-47 and :55, :60 (close(true) focuses the reading again after Enter or Esc); pianoroll/NoteListPanel.svelte:134-140 and CcListPanel.svelte:125-131 (the row gets the focus back)

What happens

Spec 3 (86-7): give a fader, pan or send the focus with Tab, move it with ↑ ↓ (← → for pan), Enter to type. After the typed value is decided with Enter (or the box is left with Esc), the box goes away and the focus is on nothing (the page body). The next ↑ ↓ do not move the fader any more; they go to the document's key handler and move the selected notes (or what else ↑ ↓ do there). The same in the params panel: ↑ ↓ choose a row, Enter types, Enter decides, and then ↑ ↓ no longer choose rows (the list lost the focus) but move notes.

What should happen

After the box closes by a key, the focus goes back to where the keys were, as the transport's number field (close(true) → shown?.focus()) and the note and CC lists (close(true) → the row) do. Then the keys of spec 4 ("Move a fader / send ... when it has focus", "In the parameters: choose the row above / below") keep working without pressing Tab or the row again.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run.

  1. Open the mixer, Tab to a track's fader, press ↑ once (it moves), then Enter.
  2. Type -6 and Enter: submit sends setGain, typing becomes false, the ValueField and its input go away; document.activeElement is the body.
  3. Press ↑: no keydown on the fader; keys/attach.ts gets it in the "main" context and runs nudge (moves the selected notes a semitone when the piano roll has a selection).

Params panel: choose a built-in effect's row, Enter, type a value, Enter, then ↓: the row does not change.

Cause

ValueField's parents only switch typing off; none of them puts the focus back on the fader / knob / send element or on the list box, unlike NumberField and the note list which do it on key close.

Suggested fix

When the box closes from Enter or Esc (not from blur), focus the control that opened it: in mixControl keep the element that got the Enter (e.currentTarget) and focus it after typing = false; in the three params lists focus list again after stopTyping() from a key. No protocol, wording or look change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). ValueField.svelte:25-41 calls onsubmit / oncancel and is then removed; mixControl (mixer/control.svelte.ts:121-128) and paramUi.stopTyping only turn typing off; nothing focuses the fader / knob / send or the list again, so document.activeElement falls to body and the next arrow goes to the document handler. NumberField.svelte (close(true)) and the note list give the focus back.

One root with UI-SHELL-001, -002, -003, -004, -005, UI-PARITY-011, UI-PARITY-015 and UI-MIX-004: a key that belongs to the part in front (a dialog, an open list, a focused control) falls through to the document key handler (handleKeydown in ui/src/keys/attach.ts), which does not know that part is in front. Fix them as one piece of work with one rule in handleKeydown: (1) one "which dialog is in front" check used by every context (outputChooser, shelf, history) and by the Enter / Esc branches, in the order the dialogs are drawn in App.svelte; (2) return early when ev.defaultPrevented (a part that took the key says so), which covers lists moved to body; then each part only has to take its own keys.

Fix plan for this report: when the box closes by Enter or Esc (not by blur), focus what opened it: in ui/src/components/mixer/control.svelte.ts remember e.currentTarget of the Enter that opened the box and focus it after typing = false (after tick()); in ui/src/components/params/BuiltinList.svelte, SoundKnobList.svelte, PluginList.svelte focus the list box after stopTyping() from a key. Tests: Enter, type, Enter → document.activeElement is the control again.

Fix

ui/src/components/params/ValueField.svelte: the box remembers what had the focus when it opened and, after it closes by Enter or Esc (not by blur), gives the focus back to it when the focus fell to nothing (the fader, pan or send that got Enter; the parameter list). One place for every user of the box; mixer/control.svelte.ts and the lists are not changed for this. Tests: ui/tests/round5.step3.test.ts "UI-PARITY-015: after Enter or Esc in the typing box, the fader or pan has the keys again"; ui/tests/params.test.ts "UI-PARITY-015: after Enter or Esc in the typing box, ↑ ↓ choose rows again".

UI-ROLL-004lowbugfixedPiano roll drag readings count bars, beats and lengths in the song's first time signature

docs/bugs/reports/UI-ROLL-004-piano-roll-drag-readings-count-bars-beats-and-le.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:75 — meter is { num: timeline.meterNum, den: timeline.meterDen }, the time signature at the song's start only.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:285 and :1258 — that meter is what RollDrag and CcLanes (LaneMouse) read for their texts.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts:404 — draw: positionText(d.start, meter), lengthText(d.length, meter).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts:408-409 — move: signedLength(d.ticks, meter).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts:415 — resize: lengthText(…, meter).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/cc.ts:380 and :383 — CC points: positionText(…, meter).

What happens

In a song with a time signature change, the 「その場の読み値」 and the status line show the wrong bar and beat while a note is drawn or a CC point is added or moved after the change, and lengths and moves are counted in beats of the first time signature. Example: 4/4 at the start, 3/4 from bar 5 (bar 5 starts at tick 30720). Drawing at bar 7 beat 1 (tick 42240) reads 「6.3.0000」; after the release the note list and 「選んだノート」 show 「7.1.0000」 for the same note. In a 6/8 part of a 4/4 song, a note one eighth long reads 「長さ 0.0960」 while the note list says 「1.0000」.

What should happen

Spec 3, "Rules for round 3": "The value being moved (position, pitch, length, velocity, CC) is shown both in the 「その場の読み値」 near the cursor and on the status line." Everywhere else positions follow the tempo map and lengths use the time signature at that place: the note list and 「選んだノート」 (NoteListPanel.svelte:36, noteFields.ts:217, notes.ts:88), the arrange drag reading (arrangeDrag.ts:290), the marker and tempo readings (MarkerRow.svelte:302, TempoBand.svelte:78).

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run: in a 4/4 song add a 3/4 change at bar 5; open a clip that reaches bar 7 in the piano roll; choose 「書く」 and press at bar 7 beat 1, holding the button; read the reading.

Cause

positionText(tick, bars) with a plain time signature (barsMap(meter) → one meter for the whole song) counts bars of that meter from tick 0, and lengthText / signedLength take the beat of that meter. The piano roll passes the head's meter, not the tempo map it already has (map, Roll.svelte:77).

Suggested fix

Give RollDrag and LaneMouse the tempo map for their readings: positionText(tick, map), and lengths in the meter at the note (meterAtOf(map, tick)), as the note list does. No protocol change, no new wording.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). Roll.svelte:75 makes meter from timeline.meterNum / meterDen (the song's first time signature) and gives it to RollDrag (285) and CcLanes; drag.ts:404-415 and cc.ts:380-383 make the readings with positionText(tick, meter) / lengthText / signedLength. positionText with a plain meter counts bars of that one meter from tick 0. The note list and 「選んだノート」 use the map. Only songs with a time signature change are hit (hence low).

One root with UI-ROLL-005 and UI-ROLL-006: the piano roll hands its parts the first time signature (Roll.svelte:75, commands.ts:32-33) where the tempo map (map, Roll.svelte:77) is needed. Fix the three together: give RollInput / LaneInput / PianoRollKeyContext the map, and take "the meter at this tick" from it.

Fix plan for this report: ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts and cc.ts read positions with positionText(tick, map) and lengths with the meter at the note (meterAtOf(map, tick)), as NoteListPanel.svelte:36 / noteFields.ts:217 do. Test: a 4/4 song with 3/4 from bar 5, the draw reading at tick 42240 says 「7. 1. 0000」.

Fix

Fixed together with UI-ROLL-005 and UI-ROLL-006. No protocol change.

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts, ui/src/components/pianoroll/cc.ts: RollInput.meter() / LaneInput.meter() give the tempo map (Bars); positions are read with positionText(tick, map) and lengths in the time signature at the note (meterAtOf).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte, ui/src/components/pianoroll/CcLanes.svelte: hand the tempo map (mapOf(timeline)) instead of the first time signature.
  • Tests: ui/tests/pianoroll.meters.test.ts "readings while dragging follow the time signature changes (UI-ROLL-004)" (3 tests) and "the piano roll hands the tempo map to its readings (mount)".
UI-ROLL-005lowbugfixedCC points and pasted notes snap to an even grid from the song start, off the grid lines after a time signature change

docs/bugs/reports/UI-ROLL-005-cc-points-and-pasted-notes-snap-to-an-even-grid.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/cc.ts:294-297 — adding a CC point: snapTick(tick, step).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/cc.ts:408 — moving CC points: snapTick(target, step).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/commands.ts:40 — Mod V (and 「貼りつけ」 on a note) at the play line: snapTick(c.playTick, step).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:1106 — 「貼りつけ」 from the right-click on an empty place: G.snapTick(G.xToTick(g, p.x), step).
  • Compare ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:283 (note drags and the draw tool) and :1049 (pressing the ruler), which use gridTick(map, …), and ui/src/components/pianoroll/paint.ts:84-87, which draws the lines of the note area and the CC lanes from the map (eachGridTick, eachLine).

What happens

After a time signature change the grid lines are counted again from the start of each time signature part (and bar by bar for 「1 小節」), and notes snap to those lines. But a CC point that is added or moved, and notes pasted at the play line or at the right-clicked place, are put on multiples of the grid step counted from tick 0. Example: a 3/4 song with 4/4 from bar 2 (tick 5760), grid 1/2 (3840 ticks): the lines are at 3840, 5760, 9600, 13440 …; a CC point drawn at tick 9000 is placed at 7680, between two lines (the notes' snapping gives 9600). With 「1 小節」 the points and pastes go to multiples of the first bar's length, not to bar heads.

What should happen

Readings A-7: "Paste position from the right-click on an empty place: The nearest grid line (the same snapping as pressing the ruler)"; the ruler uses gridTick(map, …). Architecture 24-2: Mod V puts notes "at the play line (snapped to the grid)". CC points should land on the lines the CC lane draws, as notes land on the lines of the note area.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run: a song in 3/4 with a 4/4 change at bar 2; grid 1/2; open 「サステイン」 and draw a point near the second line after bar 2. Or put the play line there and press Mod V with notes copied.

Cause

These four places call the plain snapTick(tick, step) (geom.ts:212), which rounds to multiples of step from tick 0, instead of gridTick (time/bars.ts), which counts from the head of the time signature part and knows bar steps. LaneInput has no snapper at all (RollInput.grid has one).

Suggested fix

Use gridTick(mapOf(timeline), tick, step, false, grid.division === 0) in these places: give LaneInput a snapper like RollInput.grid, and pass the map to pianoRollCommand (it has state.timeline) and to the right-click paste. No protocol change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). cc.ts:294-297 and 408, commands.ts:40 and Roll.svelte:1106 use the plain snapTick(tick, step) (geom.ts:212, multiples of step from tick 0), while notes and the ruler use gridTick(map, …) (Roll.svelte:283, 1049) and the lines are drawn from the map. After a time signature change whose head is not a multiple of the grid step (or with the 「1 小節」 grid), the two differ. 7-readings A-7: "Paste position from the right-click on an empty place | The nearest grid line (the same snapping as pressing the ruler)".

One root with UI-ROLL-004 and UI-ROLL-006 (the piano roll uses only the first time signature); fix together.

Fix plan for this report (as suggested): give LaneInput (cc.ts) a snapper like RollInput.grid, pass the map (or the same snapper) to pianoRollCommand (commands.ts, it has state.timeline → mapOf) and use gridTick at Roll.svelte:1106. Test: 3/4 with 4/4 from bar 2, grid 1/2, a CC point at tick 9000 lands on 9600.

Fix

Fixed together with UI-ROLL-004 and UI-ROLL-006. No protocol change.

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/cc.ts: LaneInput.grid() (a snapper like RollInput.grid); adding and moving points snap with it (moveAmount takes it).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/CcLanes.svelte: gives the lane gridTick(map, …).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/commands.ts: Mod V at the play line snaps with gridTick(mapOf(timeline), …).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte: 「貼りつけ」 from the right-click on an empty place snaps with gridTick(map, …).
  • Tests: ui/tests/pianoroll.meters.test.ts "CC points and pastes land on the grid lines (UI-ROLL-005)" (3 tests).
UI-ROLL-006lowbugfixedWith the 1-bar grid, drawn notes, arrow moves, Mod D and drum squares use the first bar's length in bars of another time signature

docs/bugs/reports/UI-ROLL-006-with-the-1-bar-grid-drawn-notes-arrow-moves-mod.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:117 — step = G.gridTicks(pr.grid, meter) with meter = the time signature at the song's start (Roll.svelte:75).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/geom.ts:207-210 — for division 0 (「1 小節」) the step is ticksPerBar(meter).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts:242, :251 — a drawn note starts one step long; drag.ts:340 with geom.ts:392-394 — it never gets shorter than step while drawing.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/geom.ts:358 — a resized note cannot get shorter than one step (or its own length).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/commands.ts:33-34, :67, :74 — ← → move by step; Mod D copies by whole steps (the same head-meter step).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:124 — drum squares are step * ppt wide.

What happens

With the grid 「1 小節」 every length and move uses the length of a bar of the first time signature, also in a part of the song with another time signature. Example: 4/4 at the start, 3/4 from bar 5. In bar 6: a note drawn with 「書く」 is 7680 ticks long (a 4/4 bar), so it runs 1920 ticks into bar 7; ← → move notes by 7680 ticks, off the bar lines; Mod D puts the copy 7680 ticks later; drum squares are as wide as a 4/4 bar; a note cannot be resized shorter than 7680 ticks. The start snapping is right there (gridTick(…, bar) goes to the bar's head), so only the lengths and distances are wrong. Core's quantize uses the bar at the note (crates/meloet-model/src/quantize.rs:22-23, grid.ticks(meter) of meter_stretch(tick)).

What should happen

Spec 3: "The draw tool: pressing an empty place makes a note one grid step long"; spec 4: "Move notes by one grid step"; drums: "Its width is one grid cell minus 2". Architecture 16: "division 0 is 1 bar (from the time signature)". With 「1 小節」 one grid step is the bar at that place, as the snapping (time/bars.ts gridTick with bar) and core's quantize already count it.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run: a 4/4 song with 3/4 from bar 5; grid 「1 小節」; draw a note at the head of bar 6 and look at its length in the note list (it reads one 4/4 bar, 「4.0000」 in 3/4 beats); select it and press →.

Cause

gridTicks is given only the head's time signature (Roll.svelte:75, commands.ts:33), so for division 0 the step is always the first bar's length.

Suggested fix

For division 0 take the step from the bar at the place: the drawn start for draw and resize, the earliest moved note for ← → and Mod D, the view's left edge (or each square's bar) for drum squares, e.g. ticksPerBar(meterAtOf(map, tick)). No protocol change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). Roll.svelte:117 step = G.gridTicks(pr.grid, meter) with the first time signature (75); gridTicks (geom.ts:207-210) gives ticksPerBar(meter) for division 0, so with 「1 小節」 every length and distance (drawn note, shortest resize, ← →, Mod D in commands.ts:33, drum square width) is the first bar's length everywhere. Other grids do not depend on the meter, so only 「1 小節」 in a song with a time signature change is hit (low). Rust's own grid uses the bar at the place (crates/meloet-core/src/play/automation.rs on_grid, meloet-model/src/quantize.rs).

One root with UI-ROLL-004 and UI-ROLL-005; fix together (the map instead of the first meter).

Fix plan for this report: make the step a function of the place for division 0, ticksPerBar(meterAtOf(map, tick)): in ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts (the drawn start for draw and resize), commands.ts (the earliest moved note for ← → and Mod D), and Roll.svelte:124 (drum squares: the bar of each square, or of the view's left edge if one width per view is needed by the drawing). Check what Rust does with the sent ticks (it takes them as sent), so the screen alone decides. Tests with a 4/4 → 3/4 song.

Fix

Fixed together with UI-ROLL-004 and UI-ROLL-005. No protocol change.

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts: RollInput.step(tick) gives one grid step at a place; the draw tool takes it at the drawn start (length and shadow), resize at the grabbed note.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte: stepAt(tick) = gridTicks(grid, meterAtOf(map, tick)) for the mouse; drum squares are one cell of the bar at the view's left edge (DrumGeom.hitW is one width per view).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/commands.ts: → and Mod D take the step at the earliest selected note; ← the step just before it (from a bar's head, the length of the bar before), so a note on a bar line lands on a bar line.
  • Tests: ui/tests/pianoroll.meters.test.ts "the 「1 小節」 grid in bars of another time signature (UI-ROLL-006)" (2 tests). The drum square width has no test.
UI-ROLL-007lowbugfixedShowing a clip of another song with the same clip id keeps the old zoom, scroll and rows

docs/bugs/reports/UI-ROLL-007-showing-a-clip-of-another-song-with-the-same-cli.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:489-503 — the fit on opening (zoom, scroll to the clip, then the rows to the notes) runs only if (prView.shownClip !== clip.id).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/view.svelte.ts:40 — prView.shownClip is module state kept while the app runs; nothing clears it when another song comes (grep shownClip finds only these two places).
  • crates/meloet-model/src/mixer.rs:354 — NEW_SONG_NEXT_ID = 4: every new song gives out the same ids in the same order, and ids are per song.
  • Compare ui/src/components/pianoroll/shown.svelte.ts:283-298 and ui/src/components/pianoroll/ghosts.ts:25-28, which keep songs apart by song.serial.

What happens

After a new song (Mod N) or another song is opened, a clip shown in the piano roll that has the same id as the clip shown last in the earlier song is treated as "already open": the zoom, the sideways scroll and the top row stay as they were in the earlier song. The notes of the new clip can then be outside the view, so the piano roll looks empty, or the clip is shown at a zoom that does not fit. Clip ids repeat between songs often, because a new song always starts its ids at 4: the first clip recorded in two new songs made the same way gets the same id.

What should happen

Spec 3: "When the piano roll opens, it scrolls to the start of that clip (with the loop on and overlapping the clip, to the start of the loop)." Architecture 16, "The view range": "On opening, the largest step that fits the clip is used, scrolled to the clip start, and vertically to where the notes are." The take shown (shown.svelte.ts) and the ghost list already treat another song.serial as another song.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run.

  1. New song; record a short phrase high on the keyboard; open its clip in the piano roll and scroll far to the right.
  2. Mod N; record a short phrase low on the keyboard the same way (it gets the same clip id); press its clip.
  3. The piano roll keeps the earlier scroll, zoom and rows; the new notes are not in view.

Cause

The check compares only the clip id, and clip ids are not unique across songs. song.serial is not part of it.

Suggested fix

Keep the song serial with the shown clip (for example prView.shownClip = { id, serial } and compare both), or set prView.shownClip = null when song.serial changes. No protocol change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). Roll.svelte:489 fits only if (prView.shownClip !== clip.id); prView.shownClip (view.svelte.ts:40) is module state and is written nowhere else (grep: these two places), so it lives across songs. Ids are per song and a new song starts at NEW_SONG_NEXT_ID = 4 (crates/meloet-model/src/mixer.rs:354, song.rs:409), so the first clip of two new songs made the same way has the same id; reopening the same file gives the same ids too. shown.svelte.ts:54-74 keeps songs apart by song.serial.

Severity lowered to low: the piano roll keeps a wrong zoom and scroll once (the notes may be out of view); nothing is lost and one scroll or zoom puts it right; it is a small visual fault in the README's terms.

Fix plan (as suggested): in ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte compare the song too: keep song.serial beside prView.shownClip (a second field in view.svelte.ts) and fit when either differs. Test: mount, show clip id 5 of serial 1, then a state with serial 2 and the same clip id → the scroll is set to the clip's start again.

Fix

No protocol change.

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/view.svelte.ts: prView.shownSerial, the song serial of prView.shownClip.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte: the fit on opening runs when the clip id or the song serial differs.
  • Test: ui/tests/pianoroll.hold-mount.test.ts "fits again (zoom and scroll to the clip); the same song's clip keeps the view".
UI-ROLL-008lowparityfixedPiano roll drags, the note panels and their buttons stay usable while recording or counting in, but core ignores them

docs/bugs/reports/UI-ROLL-008-piano-roll-drags-the-note-panels-and-their-butto.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteInspector.svelte:46, :95, :180, :264-265 — live, specOf, toolUsable, canQuantize, canUnquantize look only at pr.take === take.id, not at the transport.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteListPanel.svelte:134-139 and ui/src/components/pianoroll/CcListPanel.svelte:117-122 — fields open and send setNotesField / moveControls at any time.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:280 and ui/src/components/pianoroll/CcLanes.svelte:85 — drags and the draw tool are blocked only for the clip being recorded (liveMode), not during the count-in or a recording of another clip.
  • Compare ui/src/components/pianoroll/rollMenu.ts:32 with Roll.svelte:1103 (the right-click menu disables edit rows while recording or counting in), ui/src/components/menu/menus.ts:230 (「もとの位置へ戻す」 needs !busy), and ui/src/components/Arrange.svelte:1381-1385 (no clip drag starts while busy).
  • crates/meloet-core/src/play.rs:2349-2354 (aimed returns None while busy) and :2444 (add_note returns while busy).

What happens

During the count-in, and while recording a clip other than the one shown (on another track, or a new clip on the same track), everything in the piano roll that edits notes looks usable: the buttons of 「選んだノート」 (「かける」, 「もとの位置へ戻す」, 「そろえる」, 「割合」「だんだん」「ばらつき」 「かける」, 「のばす」), its fields, the fields of 「ノートの一覧」 and 「CC の一覧」, and drags and drawing in the note area, the velocity lane and the CC lanes. A drag even shows the notes moving. On release or Enter, core does nothing (busy), nothing is said on the status line, and the notes jump back.

What should happen

Readings A-7, "Right-click during recording and count-in": "Rows that change the song (cut, paste, duplicate, delete, quantize, back to the original position) are disabled." The menu bar disables 「もとの位置へ戻す」 while busy, and the arrange starts no clip drag while busy. The rest of the piano roll should follow the same rule: the panel's buttons and fields in their disabled look, and no note or point drag or draw while busy.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run: with two keyboard tracks, open a clip of track 1 in the piano roll, select track 2 and press R. During the count-in or the recording, press 「かける」 under 「クオンタイズ」, or drag a note of the shown clip and let go.

Cause

These parts never read transport.state for count-in or recording; only the right-click menu (busy) and the clip being recorded (liveMode) are handled.

Suggested fix

Add the busy check used elsewhere (transport.state === "recording" || transport.state === "countIn", as songBusy in keys/attach.ts) to live / toolUsable / canQuantize / canUnquantize and to opening the list fields, and do not start move, resize, draw, erase, velocity or CC point drags while busy (selecting by a press or a box can stay, as copy and select all stay usable in the right-click menu). No protocol change; the disabled looks already exist.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). Rust: aimed returns None while busy() (crates/meloet-core/src/play.rs:2349-2354), and the note intents go through it; add_note returns while busy. Screen: Roll.svelte:280 blocks drags only in liveMode (135: the shown clip is the one being recorded), so during the count-in and while another clip is recorded, drags, drawing, the panel buttons and the list fields all work on the screen and are dropped by core with nothing said. The right-click menu (rollMenu.ts:32) and the menu bar already use busy. 7-readings A-7 "Right-click during recording and count-in" gives the rule (rows that change the song are disabled).

One root with UI-ARRANGE-009, UI-ARRANGE-013 and UI-PARITY-010 (parts that look usable while recording or counting in though Rust refuses). Also reported as UI-PARITY-005 (the drag half of this report, seen from the parity side: every arrange drag only chooses while busy; merged into this report and deleted). From it, more places: ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:346-360 (down), drag.ts:191-250 (RollDrag.down starts move, resize, velocity, erase and draw with no busy check), CcLanes.svelte:137-145 and cc.ts:255-270 (CC points); the siblings that only choose while busy: Arrange.svelte:1376-1385, TempoBand.svelte:99, 166, MarkerRow.svelte:65, CompBand.svelte:90, AudioClipHandles.svelte:174, 268. While busy, down should send only the selection it would send now and start no drag.

Fix plan (as suggested): one busy for the piano roll (transport.state === "recording" || transport.state === "countIn", the rule of songBusy in ui/src/keys/attach.ts), used in: NoteInspector.svelte (live, toolUsable, canQuantize, canUnquantize: the disabled looks exist), NoteListPanel.svelte / CcListPanel.svelte (do not open a field), Roll.svelte / CcLanes.svelte / drag.ts / cc.ts (no move, resize, draw, erase, velocity or CC point drag starts; selecting by press or box stays), and the piano roll ruler's loop drag (the twin of UI-ARRANGE-013). No protocol or wording change.

Fix

No protocol or wording change; the disabled looks that exist are used.

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts, ui/src/components/pianoroll/cc.ts: busy() in RollInput / LaneInput; while busy a press sends only the selection it sends now (note, edge, CC point; a box still selects) and no move, resize, draw, erase, velocity or CC point drag starts.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte, ui/src/components/pianoroll/CcLanes.svelte: give busy (recording or counting in); the piano roll ruler starts no loop drag and sends no locate while busy (the twin of UI-ARRANGE-013).
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteInspector.svelte: editable (live and not songBusy) for the fields, the change-all rows, 「割合」「だんだん」「ばらつき」, 「のばす」, 「かける」 and 「もとの位置へ戻す」; the values stay shown, the field buttons are disabled.
  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/NoteListPanel.svelte, ui/src/components/pianoroll/CcListPanel.svelte: no field opens while busy (rows still select).
  • Tests: ui/tests/pianoroll.drag.test.ts "while recording or counting in (UI-ROLL-008)" (4 tests); ui/tests/pianoroll.cc.test.ts "… while recording, a press on a point only selects it …"; ui/tests/pianoroll.panels.test.ts "while recording or counting in, the panels do not edit (UI-ROLL-008)" (2 tests); ui/tests/pianoroll.r5-notes.test.ts "while recording or counting in, no field opens (UI-ROLL-008)"; ui/tests/pianoroll.hold-mount.test.ts "while counting in or recording (UI-ROLL-008, UI-ARRANGE-013)" (2 tests).
UI-ROLL-009lowui-uxfixedThe draw tool sounds its note or chord while the song plays, records or counts in

docs/bugs/reports/UI-ROLL-009-the-draw-tool-sounds-its-note-or-chord-while-the.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts:241 (a chord stamp) and :250 (one note) — the draw branch of RollDrag.down calls i.audition.play(…) without asking i.mayAudition().
  • Compare ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts:227 and :317 (pressing or moving a note: only if (i.mayAudition())), and ui/src/components/pianoroll/audition.ts:20-21 (notesMayAudition: only when stopped).

What happens

Pressing an empty place with 「書く」 sends audition with the note (or every note of the chosen chord) also while the song plays, records or counts in, and it sounds over the song until the button is let go. Pressing or moving an existing note does not sound at those times. While recording or counting in, core does not even write the note (edits are refused while busy), but it still sounds.

What should happen

Spec 3, "Playing sounds in the piano roll": "… Nothing sounds when selecting with a box, on Mod A, when choosing a row in the list, or during play and recording." Only the keyboard column on the left is said to sound during recording. RollInput.mayAudition is documented as "Whether to play when a note is pressed or moved (only when stopped)".

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run: play the song (Space), choose 「書く」 in the piano roll, press an empty place of the note area and hold.

Cause

The two audition.play calls of the draw tool were left without the mayAudition() check that the other note sounds have.

Suggested fix

Call i.audition.play(…) in the draw branch only when i.mayAudition() is true (writing the note stays as it is). No protocol change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). drag.ts:241 and 250 call i.audition.play(…) in the draw branch with no i.mayAudition() check; the press and move branches have it (227, 317), and mayAudition is notesMayAudition(app) && !liveMode (Roll.svelte:230, only when stopped). Spec 3 line 96: "Notes: a note sounds when pressed (until released; stopped at the length of the note), each time its pitch changes while dragging, and when it is moved with ↑ ↓ (for about 0.3 s). Nothing sounds when selecting with a box, on Mod A, when choosing a row in the list, or during play and recording." Only the keyboard column is said to sound during recording.

Fix plan (as suggested): in ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts wrap the two i.audition.play calls of the draw branch in if (i.mayAudition()); check that the pitch-change audition while a drawn note is dragged (around line 340) has the same check. Test: transport playing, draw → no audition intent.

Fix

No protocol change.

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts: both audition.play calls of the draw tool (one note and a chord) only when mayAudition(); writing stays as it was. The pitch-change sound while moving notes already had the check.
  • Tests: ui/tests/pianoroll.drag.test.ts "writes the note but does not sound it (only when stopped, as pressing a note)". Changed to the right behavior: the two draw tool tests that expect a sound now set auditionOn = true (stopped).
  • docs/architecture.md 16 "Audition": the draw tool sounds only while stopped.
UI-SHELL-005lowbugfixedKey list and About open over the export dialog, and Enter in them starts the export

docs/bugs/reports/UI-SHELL-005-key-list-and-about-open-over-the-export-dialog-a.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/menu/menus.ts:452-458 (noDialog checks songDialog, settings and the scan-stopped dialog, not export.dialog nor 「書き出しました」)
ui/src/keys/attach.ts:511-541 (plain Enter with state.export.dialog sends startExport, also while the key list or About is in front)

What happens

With the export dialog (or 「書き出しました」) open, 「ヘルプ ▸ キーの一覧」 and 「meloet について」 stay usable, and the dialog opens on top of the export dialog. Then plain Enter (for example in the key list's search box, or on the 「閉じる」 button) does not reach the key list: handleKeydown sees state.export.dialog and sends startExport, so the export starts behind the key list. In 「書き出しました」 Enter sends closeExport.

What should happen

docs/spec/7-readings.md, "Who holds 「meloet について」": while another dialog is open, the way in is disabled. The comment at menus.ts:452 says the same ("Not opened while another dialog … is open (so the Esc order stays single)"). The export dialog and 「書き出しました」 are dialogs too (App.svelte:108-118).

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run.

  1. Press Mod Shift E: the export dialog opens.
  2. Click 「ヘルプ」 ▸ 「キーの一覧」: the key list opens over it.
  3. Type in its search box and press Enter: startExport is sent and the export starts.

Cause

noDialog in topMenus was written before the export dialog and 「書き出しました」 existed and does not list them; the plain-Enter handling in attach.ts does not know the screen-only dialogs (About, key list) are in front.

Suggested fix

Add state.export.dialog === null && exportedOpen(state) === null to noDialog in menus.ts (exportedOpen is in ui/src/components/export/exported.ts). No protocol, wording or looks change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). menus.ts:306: noDialog = !songDialog && !settings && !stoppedOpen; the export dialog and 「書き出しました」 are not in it, so 「キーの一覧」 and 「meloet について」 stay usable over them. attach.ts then answers a plain Enter with startExport / closeExport whatever is in front (the Enter branches do not look at about.open / keyList.open). 7-readings: "Who holds 「meloet について」 | … While another dialog is open, the menu bar is disabled".

One root with UI-SHELL-001, -002, -003, -004, -005, UI-PARITY-011, UI-PARITY-015 and UI-MIX-004: a key that belongs to the part in front (a dialog, an open list, a focused control) falls through to the document key handler (handleKeydown in ui/src/keys/attach.ts), which does not know that part is in front. Fix them as one piece of work with one rule in handleKeydown: (1) one "which dialog is in front" check used by every context (outputChooser, shelf, history) and by the Enter / Esc branches, in the order the dialogs are drawn in App.svelte; (2) return early when ev.defaultPrevented (a part that took the key says so), which covers lists moved to body; then each part only has to take its own keys.

Fix plan for this report: in ui/src/components/menu/menus.ts add state.export.dialog === null && exportedOpen(state) === null (and the latency dialog) to noDialog; and in attach.ts the plain-Enter branches do nothing while About or the key list is open (the shared "dialog in front" check gives this for free).

Fix

ui/src/components/menu/menus.ts: noDialog also needs no export dialog and no 「書き出しました」 (the latency dialog is open only with the sound settings, already in it). ui/src/keys/attach.ts: plain Enter does nothing while About or the key list is in front (dialogInFront). Tests: ui/tests/keys.front.test.ts "UI-SHELL-005: the key list and About cannot be opened over …" and "UI-SHELL-005: Enter in the key list or About over the export dialog does not export".

UI-SHELL-006lowbugfixedThe mixer band tab always says 0.0 dB, not the master fader value

docs/bugs/reports/UI-SHELL-006-the-mixer-band-tab-always-says-0-0-db-not-the-ma.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/BandRow.svelte:35-43 (count: t("band.masterDb", { db: t("common.db", { value: dbNumber(0) }) }))

What happens

The band tab 「ミキサー」 in the band tab row always shows 「マスター 0.0 dB」, whatever the master fader is set to (for example −6.0 dB, or −∞). The comment says "It stays at 0.0 dB for now", which was true before the master strip had a fader; SongView.master.gainDb (ui/src/bridge/generated/MasterView.ts) now holds the value.

What should happen

docs/spec/3-bands.md:11 (table of lower bands): "Mixer | Band tab: the value of the master fader (not the loudness. A new song is 0.0 dB)". Spec 2 "Band tab" shows 「▸ ミキサー −6.2 dB M」. docs/progress.md ("The mixer band's tab shows the master's hand value…") also takes for granted that it shows the hand value.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run. Open the mixer (M), move the master fader to −6 dB, fold the mixer: the tab still says 「マスター 0.0 dB」.

Cause

The value is a constant dbNumber(0); app.song.master.gainDb is never read in BandRow.svelte.

Suggested fix

Fill the hole from app.song.master.gainDb with the same number form the master strip uses (faderText in ui/src/components/mixer/scale.ts, or dbNumber with the −∞ text for null). No protocol change; the wording 「マスター {db}」 stays.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). BandRow.svelte:39 fills the tab with the constant dbNumber(0); band.masterDb is used nowhere else (grep), and SongView.master.gainDb (number | null) holds the fader. Spec 3 line 11: "Mixer | Band tab: the value of the master fader (not the loudness. A new song is 0.0 dB)". The note in docs/progress.md ("The mixer band's tab shows the master's hand value also while its lane moves it") is about which value, not about a constant.

Fix plan (as suggested): in ui/src/components/BandRow.svelte fill the hole from app.song.master.gainDb in the form the master strip shows (faderText in ui/src/components/mixer/scale.ts, which has the −∞ text for null); remove the "stays at 0.0 dB for now" comment; ui/tests/screen.test.ts:90 measures the tab's width with 「マスター 0.0 dB」: check the tab keeps room for the longest value (「−60.0 dB」 / −∞).

Fix

ui/src/components/BandRow.svelte: the mixer tab fills 「マスター {db}」 from app.song.master.gainDb (common.db with dbNumber, common.dbSilent for null, as the track heads do). The tab width is measured from the shown text by bandCountsShown, so a longer value drops the count by the existing rule; no fixed width to change. Test: ui/tests/followup.test.ts "the mixer tab says the master fader value (spec 3), −∞ when it is all the way down".

UI-SHELL-007lowparityfixedAbout's license search shows only the match count in headings, not matches / all

docs/bugs/reports/UI-SHELL-007-about-s-license-search-shows-only-the-match-coun.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/about/AboutDialog.svelte:137 (「音の素材」 heading count sounds.length)
ui/src/components/about/AboutDialog.svelte:162 (「書体」 heading count fonts.length)
ui/src/components/about/AboutDialog.svelte:175 (license headings g.parts.length)
ui/src/components/about/licenses.ts:97-107 (partGroups keeps only the matching parts, so the total of a heading is lost)

What happens

On the second page of 「meloet について」, while something is typed in the search box, each heading shows only how many rows match (「▾ MIT 1」), the same as when nothing is typed.

What should happen

docs/spec/3-bands.md:131: "While searching, only the headings with matches are shown open, and the count of a heading becomes 「合う数 / 全部」." The sibling search in the shelf does this already (ui/src/components/shelfList.ts:183, shelf.matchCount 「{shown} / {total}」 while searching), and spec 3:141 says the shelf works "the same as the second page of 「meloet について」".

How to trigger

Cause

The heading count is always the length of the filtered list; the unfiltered count is never passed to sectionHead.

Suggested fix

While searching, pass t("shelf.matchCount", { shown, total }) as the count, with the total from list.sounds.length, list.fonts.length and the per-license count before filtering (for example partGroups(parts, "")). The wording 「{shown} / {total}」 exists (mock:F5-ShelfSearch); if a key of its own under about. is wanted, that is new wording for the lead. No protocol change.

Verification
