AboutDialog.svelte:137, 162, 175 always show the length of the filtered list. ui/tests/about.test.ts:258 (["▾ MIT 1"] while searching) fixes today's form, but nothing in the spec or 7-readings says that form is meant (7-readings has no line on About's counts), so the spec stands and the test changes with the fix.

Wording: the form 「{shown} / {total}」 exists as shelf.matchCount (ui/src/text/ja.ts:636, source mock:F5-ShelfSearch; en.ts has it), and spec 3 line 141 says the shelf works "the same as the second page of 「meloet について」", so the same key is used; no new wording.

Fix plan (as suggested): in ui/src/components/about/AboutDialog.svelte pass t("shelf.matchCount", { shown, total }) as the heading count while searching, the totals from the lists before filtering (ui/src/components/about/licenses.ts: give partGroups the total per license, or call it once with an empty query); change ui/tests/about.test.ts:258 to the new form.

Fix

ui/src/components/about/AboutDialog.svelte: while searching, every heading count is shelf.matchCount 「{shown} / {total}」, the totals from the lists before filtering (list.sounds, list.fonts, partGroups(parts, "") per license). Not searching: the plain count as before. Test: ui/tests/about.test.ts "the search box filters. Headings with a match open and count 「合う数 / 全部」 (spec 3)" (changed from 「▾ MIT 1」 to 「▾ MIT 1 / 1」; 「▾ 書体 1 / 2」 added).

UI-SHELL-008lowparityfixedKey list lacks the metronome window keys and the Enter of 「書き出しました」 from spec 4

docs/bugs/reports/UI-SHELL-008-key-list-lacks-the-metronome-window-keys-and-the.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/keys/keyList.ts:96-118 (OTHER_ROWS: keys taken outside keys.ts)
ui/src/components/MetronomeWindow.svelte:136-145, 184-189 (← → move the volume by 1 dB; Space / Enter tap on 「タップ」)
ui/src/keys/attach.ts:534-537 (Enter closes 「書き出しました」)
docs/spec/4-keys.md:81-82 (the two rows of spec 4; line 82 was also reported as UI-PARITY-006)

What happens

The key list dialog (Help ▸ 「キーの一覧」) has no row for two key rows of spec 4: "In the metronome window: move the volume by 1 dB / tap the tempo on 「タップ」 — ← → / Space Enter" and "Close 「書き出しました」 — Esc / Enter" (Esc is covered by 「ひとつ戻る ／ 閉じる」, Enter is not). Searching the key list for 「タップ」 or 「メトロノーム」 finds only the C key.

What should happen

docs/spec/4-keys.md, rules above the table: "A key added to the app must also be added to the key list dialog (ui/src/keys/keyList.ts; round 12, G1)." Both rows are in the spec 4 table and both are built.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run. Help ▸ 「キーの一覧」, type 「タップ」: no row.

Cause

OTHER_ROWS lists the keys of the timeline, mixer, parameters, drags and boxes, but the metronome window (round 12 R3) and E9's Enter were added after it and not put in. docs/progress.md notes that nothing checks the list row by row against spec 4.

Suggested fix

Add rows to OTHER_ROWS: group "play" for the metronome window (← → ／ Space Enter) and group "file" or "play" for closing 「書き出しました」 with Enter. Needs new wording for the labels (keyList.*), so a decision of the lead / owner (the existing keyList.* labels are builder: wording approved by the owner).

Verification

Real (read, not run): OTHER_ROWS (ui/src/keys/keyList.ts:96-118) has no row for spec 4 line 82 ("In the metronome window: move the volume by 1 dB / tap the tempo on 「タップ」 — ← → / Space Enter") nor for the Enter of line 81 ("Close 「書き出しました」 — Esc / Enter"); both are built (MetronomeWindow.svelte:136-145, 184-189; attach.ts:534-537). Spec 4 line 10: "A key added to the app must also be added to the key list dialog". 7-readings G1 "Where the other keys went" leaves out only the output chooser's keys on purpose. docs/progress.md knows only that nothing checks the list against spec 4.

Also reported as UI-PARITY-006 (the metronome half; merged into this report and deleted).

needs-lead because the two rows need wording that does not exist. I looked for a fitting key in ui/src/text/ja.ts: every keyList.* label is one made-up phrase (builder:2026-11-23, listed in 7-readings G1); row() can join existing keys with 「 ／ 」, but 「音量 ／ タップ」 (metronome.volume, metronome.tap) does not say where, and nothing says 「書き出しました」 is closed.

Recommended wording (in the form of the rows there are):

  • metronome row, group 「再生と録音」 (play, where C 「メトロノーム」 is), keys ← → ／ Space Enter: ja 「メトロノーム：音量を動かす ／ テンポを手拍子で」, en "Metronome: move the volume / tap the tempo". Words from: menu.metronome 「メトロノーム」 / "Metronome"; metronome.volume 「音量」 / "Volume"; the verb of keyList.fader 「…を動かす」 / "Move …"; metronome.tapRow 「テンポを手拍子で」 / "Tap the tempo" (the owner's wording of that row); the 「名前：…」 form of keyList.shelfSample.
  • exported row, group 「曲とファイル」 (file), keys Esc ／ Enter: ja 「「書き出しました」を閉じる」, en "Close “Exported”". Words from: exported.title 「書き出しました」 / "Exported"; 「閉じる」 of button.close (spec 6) and keyList.back; spec 4 line 81 itself ("Close 「書き出しました」").

Choices: (a) add the two rows with this wording (source builder:<date> until the owner agrees, a line in 7-readings G1); (b) only the metronome row, and write in 7-readings G1 that dialog Enter keys are not listed (no other dialog's Enter is in the list; Esc is covered by 「ひとつ戻る ／ 閉じる」); (c) leave both out and say so in 7-readings G1. I recommend (a): spec 4 lists both rows and the rule above its table is plain. Files for the fixer: ui/src/keys/keyList.ts, ui/src/text/ja.ts, ui/src/text/en.ts, docs/spec/7-readings.md (G1), the key list test.

Fix

Choice (a), the lead's decision. ui/src/keys/keyList.ts OTHER_ROWS: 「曲とファイル」 「「書き出しました」を閉じる」 (Esc ／ Enter) and 「再生と録音」 「メトロノーム：音量を動かす ／ テンポを手拍子で」 (← → ／ Space Enter). ui/src/text/ja.ts, en.ts: keyList.metronome, keyList.closeExported (builder:2026-11-25, with a comment pointing at spec 7 G1). docs/spec/7-readings.md G1: the row "Rows added in the bug sweep" (decided by the lead, 2026-11-25, bug sweep UI-SHELL-008; where each word comes from). Test: ui/tests/keyList.test.ts "UI-SHELL-008: the metronome window's keys and Enter of 「書き出しました」 are listed (spec 4)".

UI-TEXT-002lowparityfixedEnglish texts with a count before a noun have no singular form (1 beats, 1 steps, 1 pts)

docs/bugs/reports/UI-TEXT-002-english-texts-with-a-count-before-a-noun-have-no.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/text/en.ts:34 (msg.countingInRest; also :33 msg.countingIn, not used now)
ui/src/text/en.ts:1140 (history.head: "{total} steps" and "The {redo} above are undone")
ui/src/text/en.ts:1141 (history.headLatest)
ui/src/text/en.ts:1142 (history.headAllUndone)
ui/src/text/en.ts:1101 (lane.now: "{count} pts")
ui/src/text/en.ts:47 (msg.scanDone: "{stopped} stopped … and were left out")
Callers: ui/src/components/StatusLine.svelte:451, ui/src/components/history/HistoryHead.svelte:14-16, ui/src/components/automation/LaneHead.svelte:119, ui/src/components/StatusLine.svelte:384

What happens

In English these texts are plain strings, so a count of 1 gives broken English: "Recording starts in 1 beats. Space to stop" (the last beat of every count-in), "1 steps · now at step 1 (the newest)" (the history band heading after the first change), "The 1 above are undone and can be redone", "Now 50 % · 1 pts" (a lane with one point), "1 stopped while being checked and were left out" (a scan where one plugin stopped).

What should happen

spec 7-readings, D4 "Numbers": "A text whose noun follows a number has a form for 1 and one for any other number (n(by, one, other) in en.ts)". 60 other texts of en.ts already do this (for example history.notes, msg.undoPending, transport.countInBars).

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run. English page: start a recording with a count-in (countInBeatsLeft goes down to 1; crates/meloet-engine/tests/tempo_map.rs:847); or make one change and open 「取り消しの履歴」; or add one automation point to a lane; or a scan in which one plugin stops.

Cause

These keys were written as plain strings in en.ts, not as n(...) pairs. The test in ui/tests/text.test.ts checks holes, not the singular.

Suggested fix

Make each an n(by, one, other) pair in en.ts: msg.countingIn/msg.countingInRest by beats ("1 beat"), history.head/headLatest/headAllUndone by total ("1 step"; history.head also needs the redo part to read for 1, for example "The {redo} above is undone and can be redone", which needs the pair by redo or a rewording that works for both), lane.now by count ("1 pt"), msg.scanDone by stopped ("was left out"). English wording only; no Japanese change, no protocol change. history.head has two counts in one text, so the lead may want a rewording that needs only one pair.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). The keys named are plain strings in ui/src/text/en.ts (33-34, 47, 1101, 1140-1142), and each can get the count 1 (the last beat of a count-in, the first step, one point, one stopped plugin). 7-readings D4: "Numbers | A text whose noun follows a number has a form for 1 and one for any other number (n(by, one, other) in en.ts); Japanese is unchanged." 61 other texts use n(...).

Fix plan (English only, the forms the rule asks for; no Japanese change): in ui/src/text/en.ts

  • msg.countingIn, msg.countingInRest: n("beats", "… in {beats} beat. …", "… in {beats} beats. …");
  • history.headLatest, history.headAllUndone: n("total", "{total} step · …", "{total} steps · …");
  • history.head has two counts and n takes one: pair by total and word the second half so it reads for any number, for example "… now at step {current}. Undone above: {redo}, can be redone" (the lead or owner may choose other words; the English table is the developers' translation approved by the owner, so tell the owner in the fix's list of wording changes);
  • lane.now: n("count", "Now {value} · {count} pt", "Now {value} · {count} pts");
  • msg.scanDone: n("stopped", "… {stopped} stopped while being checked and was left out. …", "… were left out. …").

Add the singular cases to ui/tests/language.test.ts. Do UI-TEXT-003 first or together (the same english()).

Fix

ui/src/text/en.ts: msg.countingIn, msg.countingInRest (by beats), history.headLatest, history.headAllUndone (by total), history.head (by redo: total is at least 2 there, so only "The 1 above is undone" needed the form for 1; no rewording), lane.now (by count, "1 pt"), msg.scanDone (by stopped, "was left out") are now n(by, one, other) pairs. Japanese unchanged. English wording changes for the owner's list: "1 beat", "1 step", "The 1 above is undone and can be redone", "1 pt", "1 stopped … and was left out". Test: ui/tests/language.test.ts "English: a count of 1 takes the form for 1, also when signed with 「−」".

UI-TEXT-003lowparityfixedEnglish plural pair picks the plural for a signed -1 written with the minus sign

docs/bugs/reports/UI-TEXT-003-english-plural-pair-picks-the-plural-for-a-signe.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/text/index.ts:55 (english(): Number(values?.[entry.by]) === 1)
ui/src/components/pianoroll/drag.ts:408 (pr.readMove with keys: signed(d.keys))
ui/src/components/arrangeDrag.ts:293 (arrange.readMove with delta: barsDeltaText(...))
ui/src/components/pianoroll/geom.ts:441 (signed writes U+2212 "−")

What happens

In English, moving notes one semitone down reads "+0.0000 · −1 semitones", and moving clips one bar left reads "… (−1 bars)". Moving up or right reads "+1 semitone" / "+1 bar" correctly, so the two directions disagree.

What should happen

spec 7-readings, D4 "Numbers": a text whose noun follows a number has a form for 1; −1 is one semitone / one bar, as +1 is.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run. English page, drag a note down by one row (or press the down key with the drag reading shown), or drag a clip one bar to the left. signed(-1) is "−1" with U+2212; Number("−1") is NaN, so english() picks other. Number("+1") is 1, so the other direction is fine.

Cause

english() reads the hole's value as a JavaScript number; the screen writes signed values with the typographic minus U+2212 (spec style), which Number does not read. Also, a plain "-1" would give -1, which is not === 1.

Suggested fix

In english(), read the value after replacing U+2212 with "-" and compare its absolute value with 1 (Math.abs(Number(String(v).replace("−", "-"))) === 1). One line in ui/src/text/index.ts; no wording change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run; the JavaScript facts are plain: Number("−1") with U+2212 is NaN, Number("+1") is 1). english() (ui/src/text/index.ts:53-56) picks one only for Number(v) === 1; signed (pianoroll/geom.ts:441) writes 「−1」 with U+2212, and pr.readMove / arrange.readMove are n("keys", …) / n("delta", …) pairs (en.ts:914, 721). So "−1 semitones" / "(−1 bars)" against "+1 semitone" / "(+1 bar)".

Fix plan (as suggested): in english() read the value with U+2212 turned into "-" and compare its absolute value with 1. Check barsDeltaText (arrangeDrag.ts) gives a form Number reads after that (no spaces or units inside the hole). Test in ui/tests/language.test.ts: t("pr.readMove", { keys: "−1", … }) ends with "semitone".

Fix

ui/src/text/index.ts (english()): the value is read with 「−」 (U+2212) turned into "-", and its absolute value is compared with 1, so 「−1」 takes the form for 1 as 「+1」 does. barsDeltaText gives 「−1」 / 「−1.5」 (no units in the hole), which reads after that. Test: ui/tests/language.test.ts "English: a count of 1 takes the form for 1, also when signed with 「−」" (pr.readMove with 「−1」, arrange.readMove with 「−1」 and 「−1.5」).

UI-TEXT-004lowparityfixedRange mark and brackets built in code stay Japanese in English status lines

docs/bugs/reports/UI-TEXT-004-range-mark-and-brackets-built-in-code-stay-japan.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/format.ts:153-155 (rangeText: ${low}〜${high}; used by pianoroll/noteFields.ts:96,237,258,285 and pianoroll/ccFields.ts:144,168)
ui/src/time/bars.ts:150 (tickRangeText: 0〜…; used by valueClamp.ts:115, noteFields.ts:97,108)
ui/src/components/valueClamp.ts:79 (1〜${max})
ui/src/components/valueClamp.ts:113 (1〜${num})
ui/src/components/StatusLine.svelte:162 (${min}〜${max} for params.valueClamped)
ui/src/components/StatusLine.svelte:250 and :256 (${track}（${short}）, full-width brackets)
crates/meloet-core/src/play/params.rs:576 (format!("{}〜{}", …): the range of a plugin parameter, made in Rust and shown as is by StatusLine.svelte:158)

What happens

With the page in English the status line mixes Japanese marks into English sentences: "Beat is 1〜4, so it is now 4.", "Bars is 1〜64 …", "Tick is 0〜1919 …", "Ratio is 1〜20, so it is now 20.0 : 1.", "Mix is 0 %〜100 % …" for a plugin parameter, note-list and CC fields the same; and while recording two or more vocal tracks "● Recording · 6 Vocal（In 1）, 7 Vocal 2（In 1 · 2） · …". The ranges that come from en.ts are written "1–127", "30.00–300.00" (value.velocityRange, value.tempoRange), so the same sentence form shows two different range marks.

What should happen

All screen wording, and the marks that join it, follow the language of the page (CLAUDE.md: wording lives in ui/src/text/; architecture 25-36: joints are live bindings of index.ts that change with the language; spec 7-readings D4 "Joints"). English ranges use "–" as en.ts does; brackets are "(…)" as in en.ts (msg.recordingVocal "{track} ({input})").

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run. English page: type 9 into the beat part of the position box in 4/4 (or a tick over 1919, or 99 bars into a 「くり返す…」 box, or a too-large value into a built-in effect's row); or record two vocal tracks at once.

Cause

These strings are put together in components with literal 「〜」 and 「（」「）」 instead of a text key or an index.ts joint, so they never change with useLanguage.

Suggested fix

Add a range joint and a bracket form to the text tables (for example common.range 「{low}〜{high}」 / "{low}–{high}" and common.inBrackets 「{text}（{inner}）」 / "{text} ({inner})", or joints in index.ts next to LIST_COMMA) and use them at the listed places. For the plugin range, Rust should send the two ends (it has them as text already) and let the screen join them, which is a protocol change of ParamEnd; or the screen replaces the joint, which is fragile. Japanese output stays exactly the same. Needs two new English texts (the forms already used in en.ts); no protocol change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). The marks are written in code: rangeText (components/format.ts:153-155, 「〜」), tickRangeText (time/bars.ts:150), valueClamp.ts:79, 113, StatusLine.svelte:162, the full-width brackets in StatusLine.svelte:250, 256, and Rust's format!("{}〜{}", …) for a plugin parameter's range (crates/meloet-core/src/play/params.rs:576). They do not change with the language, while en.ts writes ranges "1–127" and brackets " (…)". 7-readings D4 "Joints" lists 「 ・ 」 and 「、」 and sentences; the range mark and the brackets were missed there, not kept on purpose.

No new wording is needed, only the two marks en.ts already uses, so confirmed:

Fix plan: add two joints beside LIST_COMMA / STATUS_JOIN (ui/src/text/ja.ts, en.ts enJoins, index.ts): the range mark (「〜」 / "–") and the bracket pair (「（」「）」 / " (" ")"); use them in rangeText, tickRangeText, the two places of valueClamp.ts, and StatusLine.svelte:162, 250, 256. Japanese output must stay the same character for character (the tests of spec 6 texts watch it). For the plugin range made in Rust: core knows the language (Play::names, architecture 25-36), so play/params.rs:576 picks the mark by it (language.pick("〜", "–")); no protocol change. (Sending the two ends apart would be cleaner but changes ParamEnd, a protocol version; not needed.) Add one line to 7-readings D4 "Joints" with the two marks.

Fix

Joints RANGE_MARK (「〜」 / "–") and BRACKET_OPEN / BRACKET_CLOSE (「（」「）」 / " (" ")") added beside LIST_COMMA (ui/src/text/ja.ts, en.ts enJoins, index.ts live bindings). Used in rangeText (ui/src/components/format.ts), tickRangeText (ui/src/time/bars.ts), the two ranges of ui/src/components/valueClamp.ts (through rangeText), and ui/src/components/StatusLine.svelte (the built-in parameter range through rangeText, the vocal input brackets). Rust: crates/meloet-core/src/play/params.rs joins a plugin parameter's two ends with self.names.pick("〜", "–"); no protocol change. Japanese output unchanged. Documents: architecture 25-36 (joints, the plugin range), 7-readings D4 row "Range mark and brackets". Tests: ui/tests/language.test.ts "the range mark and the brackets follow the language"; ui/tests/round7.vocal.test.ts "English: with monitor off, …" (the brackets); crates/meloet-core/src/play/params/tests.rs the_range_of_a_clamped_plugin_value_is_joined_in_the_language_of_the_page.

UI-TEXT-005lowparityfixedDrum row names of the piano roll lose their term tips in English

docs/bugs/reports/UI-TEXT-005-drum-row-names-of-the-piano-roll-lose-their-term.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

ui/src/components/pianoroll/drums.ts:111-115 (DRUM_TERMS, drumTerm)
ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:996 (term: drumTerm(name))
ui/src/components/pianoroll/Roll.svelte:1181 (the tip is drawn only when k.term is set)

What happens

On a drum track the row names of the piano roll (キック, スネア, クローズハイハット …) carry the term tips of spec 5 in Japanese. In English the names are "Kick", "Snare", "Closed hi-hat" …, drumTerm finds none of its Japanese words in them and returns null, so no drum row has a tip.

What should happen

spec 7-readings, D4 "Term tips": "in English a name shown in English (drum names, …) finds its term too". termParts (format.ts:79) already finds the English word of a term in English text; it is only never reached here because drumTerm gives null.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run. English page, open the piano roll on a drum track with a built-in kit (or an SF2 kit), rest the mouse on "Kick": no tip. In Japanese, resting on 「キック」 shows 「キック — いちばん低い、足で鳴らす大きな太鼓…」.

Cause

drumTerm(name) compares the shown name with the Japanese words only (name.includes(word)), but drumName gives the name through t(), which is English on an English page.

Suggested fix

In drumTerm, also match the English word of each term when the page is English: for each Japanese word, test the shown name against findTerm(word)?.term (case-insensitive, as termParts does), and return the Japanese word (the key use:term takes). No wording change; no protocol change.

Verification

Confirmed by reading (not run). drumTerm (pianoroll/drums.ts:111-115) tests the shown name against Japanese words only; the name comes through t(), so on an English page it is "Kick", "Snare" … and nothing matches; Roll.svelte:996, 1181 then draw no tip. 7-readings D4: "Term tips | Looked up by the Japanese word as before; in English a name shown in English (drum names, …) finds its term too."

Fix plan (as suggested): in ui/src/components/pianoroll/drums.ts drumTerm, when textLanguage() === "en", also test the name against findTerm(word)?.term without case (the rule termParts uses in components/format.ts:79-80), and return the Japanese word (the key use:term takes). Keep "longer first". Check the English terms of the eight words against the English kit names in en.ts ("Closed hi-hat" must find ハイハット, "Side stick" サイドスティック) in a test in ui/tests/language.test.ts.

Fix

No wording change.

  • ui/src/components/pianoroll/drums.ts: drumTerm also finds a term's English word in the name on an English page (a whole word in any case, as termParts does) and gives the Japanese word, longer words first as before.
  • Test: ui/tests/language.test.ts "the English kit names find the same terms as the Japanese ones".
UI-TEXT-006lowparityfixedTrack made by Turn this track into audio is named with a Japanese suffix in English

docs/bugs/reports/UI-TEXT-006-track-made-by-turn-this-track-into-audio-is-name.md · found 2026-11-25

Where

crates/meloet-model/src/audio/bounce.rs:17 (BOUNCED_SUFFIX = "（音）")
crates/meloet-model/src/audio/bounce.rs:158-168 (bounced_name)

What happens

With the page in English, 「このトラックを音にする」 ("Turn this track into audio") on a track named "Piano" makes a new vocal track named "Piano（音）" (and "Piano（音） 2" when taken): a Japanese word and full-width brackets in an English screen, shown in the track head, the mixer strip, the history row and the status line "Put audio clips on 2 Piano（音）…".

What should happen

Architecture 25-36 "Names made in Rust": the language is passed in where a name is made (vocal_track_name, InstrumentRef::track_name, …), and after SetLanguage what is made later follows it; spec 7-readings D4 "Names of new things" gives the English of each name made by meloet. The bounced track's name is made by meloet at that moment, like a new vocal track's "Vocal". The list of things that stay Japanese (folders in a song, file names, logs, the AI link) does not include it.

How to trigger

Read from the code, not run. 「表示 ▸ ことば / Language ▸ English」; on a keys track with a clip, right-click ▸ "Turn this track into audio"; wait for it to finish.

Cause

bounce_track names the track with the fixed BOUNCED_SUFFIX and takes no Language, unlike the other name makers of round 12 D4.

Suggested fix

Pass meloet_model::Language into bounce_track (from Play::names, as add_vocal_track gets it) and pick the suffix with language.pick("（音）", " (audio)") or another English the owner approves. Needs new English wording (spec 3 has only 「（元の名前）（音）」; spec 7-readings D4 lists the English names, so the lead or owner should choose it). No protocol or song-file change. The work file name (bounce_file_name, core play/bounce.rs:71) follows the track name and may stay as it is (file names are out of D4).

Verification

Real (read, not run): bounce_track names the new track with the fixed BOUNCED_SUFFIX = "（音）" (crates/meloet-model/src/audio/bounce.rs:17, 158-168) and takes no Language, while architecture 25-36 "Names made in Rust" passes the language in wherever meloet makes a name (vocal_track_name, add_vocal_track, import_audio …) and its list of things that stay Japanese (folders in a song, file names, logs, the AI link) does not hold this name. So an English page gets "Piano（音）".

needs-lead because the English suffix is new wording. I looked for an existing text: spec 3 (A7) has only 「（元の名前）（音）」 (「ピアノ（音） 2」 when taken); 7-readings D4 "Names of new things" lists the approved English names and has none for this; ui/src/text/en.ts has the action ("Turn this track into audio", menu.bounceTrack; "Turn into audio clips") but no name form. Rust names are not in ui/src/text/ anyway (they are picked with language.pick in the model).

Recommended wording: Japanese stays 「（音）」; English " (audio)" (a space, half-width brackets), so "Piano (audio)", and "Piano (audio) 2" when taken. Where the words come from: "audio" is the word en.ts and docs/spec/glossary.md use for 音 in this feature ("Turn this track into audio", "audio clips"); the bracket form is the one of msg.recordingVocal "{track} ({input})".

Choices: (a) pass Language into bounce_track (from Play::names, as add_vocal_track gets it) and pick the suffix; add the name to 7-readings D4 "Names of new things"; tests of bounced_name in both languages. No protocol or song-file change; old songs keep their names (a name never changes by itself). (b) keep 「（音）」 in both languages and add it to the "not changed" list of architecture 25-36. I recommend (a). The work file name (bounce_file_name, crates/meloet-core/src/play/bounce.rs) follows the track name and needs no change.

Fix

Lead's decision (a): bounce_track takes a Language (crates/meloet-model/src/audio/bounce.rs; bounced_name picks BOUNCED_SUFFIX 「（音）」 or " (audio)", same clash rule); crates/meloet-core/src/play/bounce.rs passes self.names. Songs already saved keep their names. The work file name (bounce_file_name) keeps 「（音）」 (file names are outside D4). Documents: architecture 25-36 "Names made in Rust" and A7 line, 7-readings D4 row "The name of a track turned into audio" (decided by the lead, 2026-11-25). Test: crates/meloet-model/src/audio/bounce/tests.rs in_english_the_name_ends_in_audio_with_the_same_numbers (the other callers in tests pass Language::Ja).

To look at later (not bugs, not fixed)

AI7AI — worth a look later (not bugs)
  • crates/meloet-ai/src/lib.rs:286 — an accept error is continued at once; an error that repeats (EMFILE when the process is out of file handles) turns the link's thread into a busy loop. A short sleep after an error would do.
  • crates/meloet-ai/src/tools.rs:750, tools/notes.rs:514, tools/midi.rs:392, tools/render.rs:269 — a track given as "id:7" is turned into its number on the AI thread and the ask carries the number; if the person adds, moves or deletes a track before the core runs the ask (outside a batch), the number names another track. Asks that take track ids (SetInstrument, SetPluginOutput …) do not have this window.
  • crates/meloet-ai/src/tools/automation.rs:324-373 — move_automation / delete_automation name points by index with no guard like the take id of notes; after the lane changed (the person, or another call), an old index moves or deletes another point.
  • crates/meloet-ai/src/tools/render.rs:133-149, tools/plugstate.rs:311 — scratch.clean (which may remove_dir_all folders of render_audio) and scratch.dir run on the link's one runtime thread, not on the blocking pool; a big folder stalls every connection for that time.
  • crates/meloet-ai/src/surge/sources.rs:1 — the module comment says Surge XT 1.3.x has 41 sources; SOURCES holds 40 (ids 1–40).
  • crates/meloet-ai/src/tools/midi.rs:458 — the error says the name must end in .mid, but .midi is taken too (is_midi_name).
  • crates/meloet-core/src/play/timeline.rs:405-420 — an AI rename_marker of the marker the person has just made with K (still "fresh") amends that step, the undo count does not change, and the core answers Unchanged, so the tool says "No change" though the name changed.
CORE-AUDIO11Later: CORE-AUDIO
  • crates/meloet-core/src/vocalrec.rs:157-161 — after a write_all of a voice file fails part way (no space), the next blocks are still written; if space comes back, the bytes after the short write are out of step with the frames (24-bit: noise), and frames counts less than the file holds. The recording is stopped soon after (VocalsCannotGoOn), so it is a short stretch.
  • crates/meloet-core/src/audiofiles/keeper.rs:620-637 — when the decoded copy is made but the peaks file cannot be written, finish returns an error and Done { ok: false } turns the source to Missing: the clip is shown as not found although it plays.
  • crates/meloet-core/src/readahead.rs:358-371 (with service.rs:1852-1874) — a narrow race: a file can become ready between a failed store.open and unopened.fetch_or; follow_store then sees no unopened seat and the clip stays silent until the song or the store changes again.
  • crates/meloet-core/src/audiofiles/wav.rs:331-353 — CacheWav::finish is not synced; after a power loss a renamed but short decoded copy still opens at the right rate, so playable calls it ready and the clip's end plays as silence until the cache is emptied.
  • crates/meloet-core/src/audiofiles/keeper.rs:538-546 — when Resample::new fails after CacheWav::create, the .part file is left in the cache (the early ? drops the writer without abandon).
  • crates/meloet-core/src/audiofiles.rs:429-433 — two table entries with one uid (the same bytes brought in under two names) open only the first name; when that file is missing the other is not tried, and watch reports the uid by the first name only.
  • crates/meloet-core/src/audiofiles.rs:482-491 — a save cut short while copying leaves <name>.part in the person's 音/ folder, which meloet never cleans.
  • crates/meloet-core/src/audiofiles.rs:308 — bring_in gives the copy its name with fs::rename, which replaces a file that got the same name between find and the rename; put_in_place uses hard_link to refuse that.
  • crates/meloet-core/src/readahead.rs:325-340 — a second splice that comes inside the 10 ms of the first starts from the first's new audio, so the frame before it (still part old) steps to the new audio without the overlap.
  • crates/meloet-core/src/vocalrec.rs:1000-1013 (service/input.rs:413) — DiskWriter::mark makes the core thread wait up to 300 ms (MARK_WAIT) when no block after from has come yet (R while playing with a large output delay and a fast tempo).
  • crates/meloet-core/src/audiofiles/keeper.rs:465-467 — a flac whose head says more frames than it holds gets its peaks made again at every start (the peaks file's count never equals source.frames).

CORE-FILES9Later: CORE-FILES
  • crates/meloet-core/src/songfile.rs:1981 — the temporary names .{name}.new and .{name}.prev.new add up to 10 bytes to the song file's name, and folder_name has no length cap: a song name of about 245 bytes (about 80 Japanese characters) saves once, then every later save fails with ENAMETOOLONG.

  • crates/meloet-core/src/settings.rs:403 — one field of the wrong type (e.g. "scale": "x") resets every setting (output, favorites, plugin places), and the next save writes the defaults over the file; meant by the tests, worth a look.

  • crates/meloet-model/src/automation.rs:836 — add_fade_out wakes a master lane that rests; its old points before the fade's start then move the master volume where the hand's fader was heard until now.   • crates/meloet-core/src/play/bounce.rs:93 — turning a soloed keys track into audio: the keys track is muted and stays soloed, the new vocal track is not soloed, so nothing is heard until solo is cleared (mute wins over solo). The spec says nothing about solo here.
  • crates/meloet-model/src/audio/follow.rs:65 — until writes a ramp between two equal tempos differently from a steady stretch with the same seconds, so is_the_songs says no after a ramp like that is turned off, and the clip is made again through the stretcher at a ratio of 1.
  • crates/meloet-model/src/automation.rs:380, 568 — the flat value (the hand's place) goes into points with clamp, which keeps NaN; today the plugin values it comes from seem to be filtered for non-finite values upstream, but the model's "values are never NaN" rests on that.
  • crates/meloet-model/src/audio/comp.rs:148 — while a clip has a comp, set_comp accepts a one-piece comp naming the shown take: it sounds as no comp, but played_frames stays None, so 「ノーマライズ」 and 「逆にする」 stay unavailable for that clip.
PLUG10PLUG — worth a look later (not bugs)
  • crates/meloet-plughost/src/control.rs:217-224 — close_window clears told before it lets the slot's held parameters go, so a value held back by the 33 ms limit is dropped instead of being told before ParamEnd (wire.rs:399-400 says the last value before ParamEnd is always told). Needs a close or an unload within one look of the last move.
  • crates/meloet-plughost/src/control.rs:352-365 — closing a window by its × while the person is in it sends EditorLeft but does not call controller.editor_left() as look_at_focus (404-406) does, so VST3's re-sync after leaving (architecture 20-14) is skipped for this way of leaving. No plugin seen that needs it there.
  • crates/meloet-plughost/src/formats/vst3.rs:987-997, 1167-1176 — values the person moves in a VST3 window (performEdit) come back to the controller with setParamNormalized after their block; during a drag in the plugin's own window an echo of an older value may move its knob back for a moment, depending on the plugin. Not seen.
  • crates/meloet-plughost/src/rt.rs:135-143, 156-164 — one block takes at most 512 changes from the main thread (to_plugin_events + COMMANDS); an LV2 window that writes more ports at once (a preset loaded by the window itself) loses the rest: the DSP never gets them while Shared::values shows them.
  • crates/meloet-plugin/src/ipc/ends.rs:211-216 — block_ms is whole milliseconds; with blocks under 1 ms (32 frames at 48 kHz) it is 0 and any request a host thread holds counts as long, so an overdue answer is never slept for (one more silent block each time).
  • crates/meloet-engine/src/plugin.rs:198-208 — push_event returning false is ignored; a host late for many blocks (a plugin loading a patch inside its block for a second or more) fills the 256-record ring and later events, note-offs too, are lost: possible stuck notes.
  • crates/meloet-plughost/src/control.rs:171 — Load refuses a max_frames larger than the mapping's but takes a smaller one, while the engine's blocks go up to the mapping's size; VST3 buffers sized by the activation would then overflow. Core always sends the mapping's size today.
  • crates/meloet-plughost/src/window.rs:297-298 — when the X connection breaks (Xwayland gone), events stops reading but the fd stays in poll and keeps signalling hang-up, so the host's main loop spins.
  • crates/meloet-plughost/src/control.rs:1149-1152 — a reply whose text is over 16 MiB or blob over 256 MiB fails write_frame before anything is written, yet the host then ends; an Error reply would keep the plugin running.
  • crates/meloet-plugin/src/scan.rs:148-149 — one unreadable folder inside a bundle makes BundleStamp::read fail, and places_todo (scanner.rs:270) then never scans that bundle, without a word.
PROTO8Later: PROTO (crates/meloet-protocol)
  • crates/meloet-protocol/src/timeline.rs:3 — the module comment says a tempo is beats of the meter's lower number a minute; CLAUDE.md and the rest of the code say quarter notes a minute whatever the meter. Misleading comment in the contract.
  • crates/meloet-protocol/src/lib.rs:754 — Command::Audition says the screen sends AuditionOff; the screen sends audition with empty keys instead (ui/src/components/pianoroll/audition.ts:78), so Command::AuditionOff has no sender.
  • crates/meloet-protocol/src/lib.rs:1527 — Command::TypedKey says the screen turns the key time into Rust's clock with HelloReply::mono_us; it uses clockOffsetUs (ui/src/keys/typed.svelte.ts:69), and HelloReply.monoUs is read by nothing.
  • crates/meloet-protocol/src/history.rs:485 — StepWhat::Points is never made by core (control points use StepWhat::ControlPoints), but stays in the contract and in ui/src/components/history/stepText.ts:90.
  • crates/meloet-protocol/src/lib.rs:3083 — KeyboardView.port_name, address, EndpointView.node_name, AudioView.forced_quantum, output_device, midi_input are sent with every change of their sections and read by no screen part (only tests and measure.rs); architecture 3-2 lists them as screen content.
  • crates/meloet-protocol/src/lib.rs:3824 — StatusMessage::AudioImported.skipped (and count) are not shown: when some chosen files do not read, the status line names only the first file brought in, and the person is not told files were left out. Spec 6 has no sentence for it (needs wording from the owner).
  • crates/meloet-protocol/src/lib.rs:383 — the old shelf commands ShelfSelect, PlaySample, ToggleFavorite, ShelfSelectEffect are only reached through their …Sound / ChooseEffect forms inside core; nothing on the screen or in the AI link sends them directly any more (they could be kept internal to core).
  • crates/meloet-core/src/service.rs:1410, 1452-1457 — every keyboard CC and pitch bend is sent as Event::Control / Event::Bend on the messages Channel (as architecture 3-1 item 6 says), but no screen listener reads them (ui/src/gl/engine.ts:342 and the others return unless note); the screen shows pedal, modulation and bend from the telemetry. Not a fault a person meets; a wheel moved for a while makes hundreds of unread JSON messages a second. Either stop sending them and change 3-1, or use them (was report PROTO-001, taken out by the verifier).
SOUND7SOUND — worth a look later (not bugs)
  • crates/meloet-sound/src/synth/poly.rs:535 — a held note's filter_env is multiplied once per 32-frame chunk with no floor; after 11–90 s of one held note (bass, lead, pad) it sits at a denormal number. One operation per chunk per voice, so the cost is negligible; only the rule of flushing tiny values in loops.
  • crates/meloet-sound/src/sampler/player.rs:476-482, 223-227 — a pack region's semitones are not clamped (only SF2 shapes are); instrument.json allows pitchKeytrack up to ±1200 %, and a key far from root then gives a step so big that last + 3 in mix overflows (debug panic, or in release a wrong fast path whose slice index panics) on the audio thread. Packs are made by tools/sounds/build.py, so not reachable today; a clamp like MAX_SHAPED_SEMITONES for all regions would close it.
  • crates/meloet-sound/src/sampler/player.rs:35, 709-714 — the comment of MAX_SHAPED_SEMITONES says a shaped voice is never further than 120 semitones from its file, but the modulation envelope, vibrato and LFO cents (each clamped to ±12 000) are added per chunk on top, up to ±360 semitones. No crash on 64-bit; the comment or the clamp could be made to agree.
  • crates/meloet-sound/src/phrases.rs:229-251 — PhrasePlayer does not pass ask or latency on and cuts each block at note frames; on the sample seat with a plugin (service.rs:3691) every piece is its own synchronous round trip to the plugin host, never asked ahead.
  • crates/meloet-sound/src/sf2/zones.rs:261-311 — the work is capped only by the number of zones made: a damaged preset with up to 16 384 local zones whose instruments each have up to 16 384 zones that all fail (sample out of range, no key overlap) loops about 2.7e8 times and builds a 16 384-zone Vec per preset zone; the SF2 load (and keys, key_names) can then take minutes on its thread.
  • crates/meloet-sound/src/sf2/mod.rs:160-169 — Sf2File::keys takes key_lo.min(127), so a zone whose key range lies wholly above 127 (damaged file) marks key 127; it also counts zones spec would drop (ROM sample, bad rate, sample out of range). key_names skips key_lo > 127 but not the unplayable ones.
  • docs/architecture.md 25-23 (last line) — names Gate::gain and DeEsser::cut_db as if they could be read; in crates/meloet-sound/src/effects/gate.rs and de_esser.rs they are private fields with no accessor.

  • ui/src/gl/Surface.svelte:72-76 — the $effect reads current.state, so engine.setScale runs after every patch of any section; setScale calls touch(null), which marks every region dirty, so each patch re-measures every slot box (getBoundingClientRect) and rebuilds every picture even when the scale did not change. Only a cost; the picture comparison stops the redraw.

  • ui/src/bridge/peaks.ts:152-175 — more() of a growing level copies every peak it has into a new Int16Array on each poll (WAVE_POLL_MS, 10 a second), so a long recording copies more and more each poll (a 10-minute stereo take at level 0 is about 0.9 MB a poll).
  • ui/src/gl/pool.ts:139-143 — a canvas given back to the pool keeps its wave textures (up to WAVE_TEX_KEEP_BYTES, 16 MB of GPU memory per canvas) until another region takes it and a picture goes over the limit.

A design question, not a fault seen in use.

  • ui/src/lib/HScrollbar.svelte:3-5 — the header comment says the handle is gray.8 on hover and gray.9 while moving; the CSS (and 7-readings "Fill or ink, per place") uses accent and accent.ink. Stale comment.
  • ui/src/dev/watch.ts:47 — getContext("webgl2") on every canvas of the page: a canvas with no context yet would get a new WebGL context (counted in the budget). Today every canvas in the page has one (WebGL or bitmaprenderer), so nothing is made; development only.
